Dialog, AlertDialog, Sheet, Drawer and Tooltip popups draw a CanvasText outline where forced colours replace their fill and remove their shadow, and the blanket and a parent's dimming stay a veil of Canvas. See Components/Overlays Modal and Components/Tooltip Icon Buttons in storybook-forced-colors.
- **Footers sit on the overlay's own surface** (audit G6-2). DialogFooter, SheetFooter, AlertDialogFooter and DrawerFooter paint `utility.elevation.surface.current` (the overlay surface) below their hairline instead of `elevation.surface.sunken`, which merged with the blanket in dark mode. SheetFooter's inline inset is `space.200`, as SheetHeader's and SheetBody's are (MOD-14). See Components/Dialog Scrollable.
- **Titles and descriptions wrap unbroken values** (audit G1-1). The Dialog, AlertDialog, Sheet and Drawer title and description break a code, a hash or a constraint name inside the popup instead of running past its edge. See Components/Dialog Long Values and Components/AlertDialog Long Values.
- **SheetPortal and SheetOverlay are exported** (audit MOD-14), as Dialog's and AlertDialog's are, for a custom popup at an edge. See Components/Sheet Custom Portal.
- **Popover and HoverCard stay on screen** (audit FLT-1, G5-10). Both are capped at the height the window leaves on their side (`var(--available-height)`) and scroll within it, so a calendar on a landscape phone keeps its month caption and arrows in view. See Components/Popover Short Window.
- **Anchored overlays take the positioner's props** (audit FLT-2). PopoverContent, HoverCardContent and TooltipContent take `anchor` (an element, a ref or a virtual point, such as a chart mark or a cell), `collisionPadding`, `collisionBoundary`, `collisionAvoidance`, `sticky` and `positionMethod`, beside the four placement props. See Components/Popover Anchored.
- **HoverCard centres on its trigger** (audit FLT-11, G3-16). `alignOffset` defaults to 0, not 4, and HoverCardTrigger draws the kit's focus ring. The Overlays page records which parts set `font.body.small` and which `font.body`. See Components/HoverCard Basic and Hover Card Matrix.
- **Tooltip timing is the product's by default** (audit FLT-10, the tooltip half). TooltipProvider defaults to `delay={300}` and `timeout={300}`; the Overlays table says keyboard focus opens a tooltip at once. See Components/Tooltip Icon Buttons.
- **A tooltip can describe its trigger** (audit FLT-9). `describe` on TooltipContent renders a visually hidden copy, always on the page, as the trigger's accessible description, for a shortcut the name leaves out; the page shows `aria-keyshortcuts` beside it. See Components/Tooltip Shortcut.
- **Escape on an unavailable trigger closes only its tooltip.** A Button or IconButton with `disabledReason` (`aria-disabled`) loses the trigger's own key handling, so Escape reached the dialog around it and closed that too; TooltipTrigger now closes the tooltip and keeps the key. Tooltip.mdx documents `disabledReason` for unavailable actions, without the focusable wrapper or the `title` (audit FLT-4's docs half, FLT-12). See Components/Tooltip Unavailable.
- **A focused field scrolls clear of the footer in a short window** (audit G5-3, OVERLAYS). Under 30rem tall a Dialog, AlertDialog, Sheet or Drawer scrolls as one under its sticky footer; the footer now gives the popup its measured height as `scroll-padding-block-end`, so a field that takes focus lands above the footer instead of under it, as RecordBrowser and PickerSheet already did for theirs. See Components/Dialog Short Window and Zoomed Window.
- **A tooltip sits on the top layer** (audit TOK-14's tooltip half, OVERLAYS). TooltipContent's positioner takes `layer.tooltip` in place of z-50, so a tooltip shows above a toast, such as a toast action's label. See Components/Tooltip Icon Buttons.

- **DataTable chooses one record with a radio** (audit DTC-2, DTP-3, PIK-11). `selectable: "single"`, or `selectable: true` beside `enableMultiRowSelection: false`, draws a native radio per row and no select-all: Tab reaches the chosen radio, the arrow keys move the choice, and a click on the row chooses it when the table opens nothing. `value` and `onValueChange` on useDataTable own the chosen id. See Patterns/Data table Choosing one.
- **A row is keyed by its record's `id`** (audit DTC-15). Without `getRowId`, rows whose records all have a unique `id` are keyed by it, so a refresh never moves a selection or an open row to another record; otherwise they are keyed by position, and the hook says so in the console for a table that selects, opens details or nests. It also warns when `virtualize` meets `groupBy` and when `initialState.columnPinning` replaces the columns' own pins (DTC-16). See Patterns/Data table Choosing one.
- **The table holds its preview** (audit DTC-13). `preview: { onPreview, activeId }` on useDataTable puts the eye on each row's first value and marks the open row, as `c.id`'s `preview` and `active` do, so a register's columns can stay at module level. `displayedRows(table)` and `showRow(table, id)` let a preview's previous and next walk every row the search and filters leave, across pages (D6). See Patterns/Data table Preview held by the table.
- **DataTable names each eye, each resize handle and, on request, each row** (audit TBL-1, DTC-1, TBL-10, the DataTable half). The eye says "Preview FND-2200" from the row's label, the tree's or the value beside it; each resize handle says its column ("Resize Status"); `rowHeader` on useDataTable draws that column's cell as `th scope="row"`. See Patterns/Data table Preview held by the table.
- **Headings align with their figures** (audit TBL-2, TBL-3, A11-11, the DataTable half). DataTable passes each column's `align` to Table.Header, Table.Cell and the footer, and centres a column group's heading with the prop, so an end-aligned heading ends over its numbers, and in a right-to-left table on the reading end. See Patterns/Data table Tree.
- **Responsive fitting follows priority and gives the slack to the unsized columns** (audit DTP-4, DTP-8). The first column that does not fit folds, and every column after it, so a lower-priority column never outlasts a higher one. Once everything fits, the author's widths hold and the columns left unsized share the spare width in proportion to their minimums, through custom properties set outside React, so a resize still redraws no row; with every column sized the identity takes it. The kinds record whether the author gave a width (`meta.sized`); an id column counts as sized unless it has a `minWidth` and no `width`, so a code keeps its width and a name given only a minimum grows. See Patterns/Data table Unsized columns take the slack.
- **Stored views carry the author's version** (audit DTC-6). `view: { id, version, scope }`: raising `version` discards every layout stored under an older one, and `scope` keeps two readers in one browser apart. A column added since a layout was stored takes the author's place, visibility and pin, a column the reader cannot hide always shows, and the layout applies before the first paint. `readView` and `writeView` take the version. See Patterns/Data table Versioned views.
- **Refreshing keeps the rows, and a load is said** (audit DTC-7, DTC-10, API-12). `state="refreshing"` keeps the rows under `aria-busy` with a quiet bar on the header's edge; a load or refresh that outlasts half a second is said ("Findings, loading"). A server page that comes back empty keeps the toolbar, the header and the pager, with Go to first page. A virtual window or a server page carries `aria-rowcount` and each row its `aria-rowindex`. See Patterns/Data table Refreshing and a stranded page and A virtual window.
- **A compact empty, fewer skeletons and no pager for one page** (audit VW3-13, EMP-12). `empty.size: "compact"` with `empty.icon` says there is nothing in one short row, for a collection of a few rows in a record; `loadingRows` sets the skeleton rows (the page size, at most ten, unsaid); the pager hides while every row fits the smallest page size. See Patterns/Data table Compact empty.
- **Clear filters keeps focus in the table** (audit handoff, TLB-4). After Clear filters, or Go to first page, focus is in the search that asked, else the first control of the rows now shown, else the table's first control. The search starts as empty text, so a Toolbar bound to it is controlled from the start. See Patterns/Data table States.
- **An editable DataTable stays a table** (audit DTC-8, the grid half). A table with an `editable` column no longer sets `role="grid"`, which promised arrow-key cell navigation it did not have: Tab moves across its cells, Enter commits and moves down the column, and a screen reader's own table keys read it. See Patterns/Data table Editing.
- **Rows open like links** (audit DTC-14, DTC-8). `onRowClick` gets the click as its second argument and is not called for a click on a control or a link in the row, or one that ends a text selection; Cmd, Ctrl, Shift or the middle button open `rowHref`, else the row's first link, in a new tab. A focused tree row opens on Enter. See Patterns/Data table Rows open like links.
- **Row actions can navigate** (audit DTC-11). A RowAction with `href` (and `render` for a router link) is a link item; each takes `icon`, `description`, `disabledReason` and `group`, with a separator between groups, and `id` keys it. The menu sizes to its longest label. See Patterns/Data table Row actions that navigate.
- **Exports and totals take every row the filters leave, and CSV cells never run** (audit DTC-12, DTP-13). `toRows` and `toCsv` export a tree's folded parts with each row's level and a group's rows without their heading; a CSV value that starts with a formula's sign and is not a plain number is written as text (`keepFormulas` to keep it); `downloadCsv(table, filename)` saves it with a byte order mark. A footer total counts a tree's rows with no parts (`footerRows: "top"` for top-level rows), so it no longer moves as a row opens. See Patterns/Data table Export and totals.
- **A date range filters by the reader's day** (audit DTP-14, DAT-9, the filter half). An instant counts on the day it falls on in the LedgerProvider's time zone, both ends inclusive. See Patterns/Data table Days in the reader's zone.
- **SelectionBar counts what the search hides and hands focus back** (audit DTC-9, DTP-11). The count says how many chosen rows the search or filters hide ("2 selected, 2 hidden by the search or filters"), a server table counts the chosen ids of every page, the count is said as it changes, and Clear or Escape in the bar moves focus to the select-all. The reference story draws the bar in the toolbar slot in place of the Toolbar. See Patterns/Data table Selection in the toolbar.
- **groupBy is the grouping** (audit DTP-15). The grouping follows `groupBy` however often it changes, and each new grouping opens its groups; `state.grouping` and `initialState.expanded` are no longer needed beside it. `initialDetails` says which detail rows start open. See Patterns/Data table Group by menu.
- **The id's glance adds no tab stop** (audit FLT-7, G3 focus). The glance trigger is no longer a focusable span; the eye and the preview hold the same facts, and a name link in the id opens it on focus. See Patterns/Data table Register.
- **A row's status is the xsmall badge** (audit STS-13). `c.status` and its Editable draw the 16px badge every row status uses. See Patterns/Data table Status map.

- **ComboboxInput's chevron leaves the tab order** (audit SEL-1, SEL-20). The chevron is Base UI's trigger rendered as the InputGroupButton, so it keeps `tabIndex={-1}` and `data-slot="combobox-trigger"`: Tab moves from the input to the next field, and nothing Base UI hides while the list is open can take focus. It is named by the Field's label, or by the input's `aria-labelledby` or `aria-label` outside one ("Show options" when the input has no name). See Components/Combobox Defaults.
- **Typing then Enter chooses the first match** (audit SEL-5). Combobox turns Base UI's `autoHighlight` on, so the first match is highlighted as the reader types; `autoHighlight={false}` leaves the highlight to the arrow keys. See Components/Combobox Defaults.
- **The Combobox popup takes its anchor's layout width** (Combobox In Dialog flake). ComboboxContent measures the input group (or `anchor`, when it is an element or a ref) without transforms, in place of Base UI's on-screen `--anchor-width`. Opened while its dialog was still scaling in, the popup followed every frame of the entrance and a list that changed height as the reader typed resized it inside the same ResizeObserver pass, which Chromium reported as a loop; a function or virtual anchor keeps `--anchor-width`. See Components/Combobox In Dialog.
- **ComboboxList is named like its input** (audit SEL-16). Outside a Field the list takes the input's `aria-labelledby` or `aria-label`, and "Options" only when the input has no name; inside one, the Field's label as before. The list restates Base UI's `role` and `tabIndex`, so a Scroller viewport's props cannot unset them. See Components/Combobox Defaults and Searchable.
- **ComboboxLabel and ComboboxSeparator look like the Select's** (audit SEL-8). They use the shared menu heading and hairline from `menu.ts`; the separator was a transparent line. See Components/Combobox Groups.
- **Chip remove buttons say what they remove** (audit SEL-11). ComboboxChipRemove is named "Remove" and the chip's words ("Remove React"), from ComboboxChip's new `label` prop or its own text; an explicit `aria-label` still wins. See Components/Combobox Multiple.
- **ComboboxStatus counts a cut list** (audit SEL-6, SEL-12). `total` on ComboboxStatus is the number of matches before Root's `limit` cut the list, or a server's total: while the list shows fewer, the status says "Showing 50 of 312. Type to narrow the list.", and children such as a loading or failure message take its place. The page documents `limit`, a virtualized list with ComboboxList as the scroll element, and the loading, failure and more-results states. See Components/Combobox Long list, Virtualized, Async Results and Failed Search.
- **Select opens below its trigger** (audit SEL-4). SelectContent's `alignItemWithTrigger` is off unless set, and `align` is `start`: the list opens under the trigger from its start edge, at least as wide as the trigger (and 144px), and as wide as its longest option up to the 720px measure and the viewport. `alignItemWithTrigger` opts into the list over the trigger. See Components/Select Placement.
- **SelectTrigger fills its Field and never grows past its container** (audit SEL-7, G1-7). Inside a Field the trigger is full width, as Input and Combobox are; elsewhere it fits its value. It is `max-w-full min-w-0`, so a long value truncates instead of widening the page. A class or style width still wins. See Components/Select Width.
- **Long option labels wrap in Select and Combobox** (audit G1-4). Each run of text in a SelectItem or ComboboxItem sits in its own shrinkable span, so a long label or an unbroken digest wraps inside its row instead of running under the check mark and out of the list. See Components/Select Placement.
- **Option descriptions and disabled reasons** (audit SEL-17). SelectItem and ComboboxItem take `description`, a second line that is the option's accessible description, and `disabledReason`, which disables the option and shows why on the faded row, as DropdownMenuItem does. See Components/Select Descriptions and Components/Combobox Groups.
- **Select and Combobox pages** (audit SEL-19, SEL-20). Both pages gain when to use them (and when a RadioGroup, a Select or a Combobox is the part), content guidance for placeholders, option labels and empty text, the states, ArgTypes for each part, and on the Combobox page the searchable-trigger recipe; the stories use the Field binding instead of hand-wired ids, with one explicit-association example kept. See Components/Select and Components/Combobox.

- **Table headings and cells take `align`** (audit TBL-2, TBL-3, TOK-5). `align` is `start`, `center` or `end` on Table.Header and Table.Cell, logical, so a right-to-left table mirrors it: an end-aligned heading ends over its figures and leads with its sort arrow, a centred column-group heading centres, and an end-aligned cell draws tabular numerals. A `text-right`, `text-end` or `text-center` class on a header or cell does the same, so DataTable's number columns and group headings line up without a change. The table itself is tabular throughout, since `font-body` reset the base layer's numerals. See Components/Table Tree, Sorted and paged and Right to left.
- **A row can say who it is** (audit TBL-1, CHF-20's Table half). `rowHeader` on Table.Cell or Table.Id renders `th scope="row"`, drawn as a cell, so a screen reader announces the row's identity with its other cells and controls. Table.Group's band is its rows' `th scope="rowgroup"`. See Components/Table Register and Groups.
- **The preview eye is named after its row and marks the open row by more than colour** (audit TBL-1, VW1-6, G6-5, TBL-18). PreviewButton, PreviewEye and Table.Id take `label` ("Preview CTRL-0412"); Table.Id names the eye after a plain-string id unsaid, and "Preview row" stays the default elsewhere. The open row's eye sits in a `color.border.selected` ring as well as the selected fill, and `aria-pressed` is always `true` or `false`. See Components/Table Register and Table Matrix.
- **Table.Group's chevron is named by its title** (audit TBL-9). The name no longer flips between "Collapse" and "Expand"; `aria-expanded` carries the state. See Components/Table Groups.
- **Table.List is a button with the whole list as its description** (audit TBL-8, FLT-8, FLT-1's list card). Without `onOpen` the line is a button whose click, Enter or tap opens the card; "+2" is spoken "and 2 more"; every item, its meta and status, and the note are the line's `aria-describedby`; the native `title` that doubled the card is gone; the card wraps its labels and meta instead of cutting them, and scrolls within the available height. See Components/Table List line without an opener.
- **Cells wrap, and show their whole value** (audit TBL-7). `wrap` on Table.Cell and Table.Header wraps a value that must be read whole; a cell of other content than a plain string, cut by its column, takes its text as its title when the pointer arrives; Table.Id's string id is its cell's title. See Components/Table Wrapping and titles.
- **A link wraps with its cell, and a wrapped heading with its sort button** (audit TBL-7, TBL-13). A link in a wrapping cell, `wrap` or a `whitespace-normal` class, wraps with the text instead of being cut to one line, as it did before the cell's link treatment; a sortable heading with `wrap` grows its button to its lines, which align with the column, instead of spilling out of the header. See Components/Table Wrapping and titles.
- **Two header rows stick apart** (audit TBL-5). A heading in a second header row sticks below the row above it, so a column-group row is not covered once the frame scrolls; DataTable's column groups get it too. See Components/Table Two header rows.
- **Focus is kept clear of pinned columns and the sticky header** (audit G3-2 and G3-6, the Table half). The frame's scroll padding covers its pinned columns and its header (unless DataTable writes its own), so Tab scrolls a control out from under them; a link in a truncating cell is cut to the cell and draws its ring inside its edge, and a link or button inside Table.Id does the same. See Components/Table Register at 390px and Table Matrix.
- **The resize handle is named and shows the kit's ring** (audit TBL-10, G3-16). `resize.label` names the handle after its column ("Resize Control"; "Resize column" unsaid), and on keyboard focus it draws the ring inside its edge with the guide beside it. See Components/Table Resize handles.
- **The selection cell is the box's target** (audit TBL-6). A click beside the checkbox in its cell toggles it and never reaches the row. See Components/Table Register.
- **One colour for a chosen row under the pointer** (audit TBL-14). Pinned cells and the eye's slot take `color.background.selected.hovered` with their row, and a static row is no hover group, so its pinned cells no longer light. See Components/Table Register and Table Matrix.
- **A hierarchy by hand is a plain table** (audit TBL-4). Table.Tree and Table.Disclosure are documented and shown in a plain table reached by Tab, with chevrons named after the row; `role="treegrid"` is DataTable's tree mode, which has the arrow keys. A row the keyboard reaches draws the kit's ring inside its edge (DTC-8's row half). See Components/Table Tree.
- **useSort reads raw values in the reader's locale** (audit TBL-17, TBL-19). Readers return an ISO day or instant, a number, a Date or the text; text collates in the LedgerProvider's locale with numbers inside it read as numbers, and `null`, `undefined` and `""` sort last in either direction. test/table-state.test.mjs covers the sort and usePage's clamp. See Components/Table Register and Sorted and paged.
- **Forced colours mark chosen rows** (audit TBL-18, DTC-19). A selected Table.Row, and so every DataTable row with a checked box, keeps a Highlight edge inside the row; the open row's eye is Highlight as a pressed button. See Components/Table Table Matrix in storybook-forced-colors.
- **Table's chevrons and colours rest under reduced motion** (audit TOK-15, the Table half). See Components/Table Tree.
- **Table's page** (audit TBL-11, TBL-15, TBL-16, G3-14). A row's click follows a link in the row and is never the only opener; Sizes is about `density`; the pinned edge is `color.border`; every part has its generated props table (the parts are exported by name from their module); a Toolbar keeps `space.200` from the table. See Components/Table Register.

- **Read-only looks read-only** (audit INP-9, CTL-11, CTL-17). A read-only Input, Textarea, InputGroup, NumberField, SearchField or time field is the sunken surface with a dashed edge and no hover, instead of the hovered look it shared; a read-only Checkbox or Radio is a sunken, dashed box with the tick or dot in the text colour and no brand fill, and a read-only Switch that is on takes the neutral fill. Checkbox and Radio share one state recipe (boundary, hover on the box, invalid, focus, chosen, read-only, disabled), so the two cannot drift, and both roots are `inline-flex`, so a plain label keeps its text on the box's line (CTL-8). contrast.test adds the read-only pairs. See Components/Checkbox Read-only and In a plain label, Components/RadioGroup Read-only, Components/Switch Switch Matrix and Components/Input Input Matrix.
- **A label dims with its control** (audit INP-9). A FieldLabel and its asterisk dim when the control in its Field is disabled on its own or by a native `<fieldset disabled>`, as well as by the Field's `disabled`; a disabled choice in a group dims only its own label. The label's `peer-disabled` classes, which never matched, are gone. See Components/Field Disabled.
- **Choice cards name the control once and draw one ring** (audit CTL-18). Inside a card (a FieldLabel around a Field) FieldTitle names the control on its own, so the FieldDescription describes it instead of also becoming its name; the card draws the keyboard focus ring and the Checkbox, Radio or Switch inside draws none, in forced colours as well; a chosen card's hint stays neutral (`color.text.subtle`) instead of turning blue. FieldTitle's slot is `field-title`. See Components/Checkbox Choice card and Components/RadioGroup Choice card.
- **Switch takes the control scale** (audit CTL-16). `size` is `medium` (32 × 20px, the default) or `small` (24 × 16px), and `data-size` reports that word; `sm` and `default` stay as deprecated spellings for one version and render as before. `SwitchSize` is exported. See Components/Switch Switch Matrix.
- **`controlBase` and `controlHeight` are deprecated** (audit CTL-17). They are the kit's own class strings, not parts; they stay exported for one version and then become internal. Compose Input, Textarea or InputGroup, or give a control `size`.
- **Fewer unnamed groups** (audit INP-16). InputGroupAddon is no longer `role="group"`, and Field and InputGroup take the role only when they are given a name (`aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`), so a search box is one searchbox, not three nested unnamed groups. This departs from shadcn's markup. See Components/InputGroup and Components/Field.
- **DateTimeField holds Enter on half an entry** (the REFFORM handoff behind product-patterns' unsettled moment). Enter in the day or the time input with only one of them filled says "Enter both a date and a time." and does not submit the form around the field; focus stays where the reader is typing. Enter on a day or a time that does not read, or on a moment outside `min` and `max`, is held the same way under the field's own message, so the form never submits an empty moment while the field shows what fixes it. See Components/DateTimeField Enter with half an entry.
- **The choice and form pages say when not to use a part** (audit CTL-6, CTL-9, CTL-10, CTL-12, CTL-13, CTL-15, INP-6, INP-10, INP-17, PRM-6). Checkbox, RadioGroup and Switch lead with the Field composition, list when not to use each control, keep names stable, never disable a control to state a fact, and put forced colours under Accessibility; RadioGroup says a controlled group with no answer passes `""`, never `undefined` (Controlled with no answer). The Switch Settings story applies each change at once, and native form values move to Native form. Forms' Layout story collapses by the form's own width (a FieldGroup container and `col-span-6 @md/field-group:col-span-N`), its first field takes focus through `initialFocus`, its primaries show `isLoading` rather than `disabled`, and its choice table names SearchField, NumberField, DateTimeField, TimeField and DateRangePicker. InputGroup says DOM order is focus order. Field documents the FieldSet as a saving lock that never widens its frame (Saving lock). See Patterns/Forms Layout, Components/Switch Settings and Components/Field Saving lock.
- **Textarea grows, and its limit never cuts a paste** (audit INP-8, INP-18). `rows` is the box's height in lines, three by default, with no 64px floor above it, so `rows={2}` is two lines. `autoResize` grows the box with its text from `rows` to `maxRows`, where it scrolls (`field-sizing: content`, measured where the browser lacks it); InputGroupTextarea takes both. `characterLimit` is a soft limit: a count under the box ("1,880 characters left", "12 characters too many" in the danger colour, with the box's edge) describes the box, is announced when the reader pauses typing, and fails native validation with its words, while the text stays whole; prefer it to `maxLength`. New messages: `characterLimit`, `charactersLeftOne`/`Other`, `charactersOverOne`/`Other`. `InputGroupTextareaProps` is exported. See Components/Textarea Rows, Auto resize and Character limit.
- **Input and InputGroup pages list their props** (the docs gap `ds:check` reports). Input.mdx renders `<ArgTypes>` for Input, and InputGroup.mdx for InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupButton, InputGroupInput and InputGroupTextarea, so neither page is on `ds:check`'s list of pages without generated props. See Components/Input and Components/InputGroup, Props.

- **Rings inside a scroller are drawn inside their box** (audit NAV-1, TLB-2's Scroller half, VW1-1, G3-3, G3-4, G3-15, DSC-1, G3-1 in part). A Scroller stamps the element it scrolls (its ScrollerViewport, or the ScrollArea viewport a Tabs strip passes it) with `data-scroller-viewport`, and scroller.css draws every focus ring inside it with the `outline-field-focused` geometry: the viewport's own ring, an item's outline and a ring an item draws on `::before` or `::after`, as Stepper and Timeline steps do. A focused saved view, saved question, line or filled tab and step now shows its whole ring. A horizontal ScrollerViewport keeps `space.025` of padding, so a resting button's ring and shadow show whole too; the strip is 4px wider and taller than its content. See Components/Scroller Horizontal and Keyboard Stop, and Components/Tabs Responsive Widths.
- **ScrollerViewport is a tab stop only when the keyboard needs it** (audit NAV-5, G3-15). While it overflows and holds nothing the keyboard can reach (no link, button or field, and no option, menu item, tab, tree item or grid cell), it takes `tabIndex={0}`, a `group` role and the name "Content, scrolls" (`contentScrolls`) unless the caller names it or gives a role; a strip that fits or holds a button adds no stop. A caller's own `tabIndex` still decides, and a focusable viewport the caller did not name takes the default name. See Components/Scroller Keyboard Stop.
- **The current item stays in view as a strip grows** (audit VW2-2, NAV-3). Until the reader scrolls, wheels, swipes, clicks, types or moves focus in a horizontal strip, the selected tab, current step or pressed view is revealed on every change of size, so counts that load after a `?tab=` deep link no longer push it off a phone's strip; afterwards only narrowing brings it back. A current item chosen from outside the strip scrolls in. See Components/Scroller Keeps Current In View and Components/Tabs Late Counts On A Phone.
- **A long vertical list keeps its native scrollbar** (audit SEL-6, the Scroller half; NAV-16). Past five viewports of content a vertical Scroller shows no arrows and leaves the native scrollbar the reader can drag, since hovering to the end of a 546-option list took half a minute. The Scroller observes only the children that come and go instead of re-observing every child on each change. See Components/Scroller Long List.
- **Scroller arrows paint the current surface** (audit G6-1, the Scroller half). The `default` surface is `utility.elevation.surface.current`, so an arrow inside a Card, Dialog or Sheet matches it in dark mode; on a page nothing changes. See Components/Scroller Horizontal.
- **The filled Tabs strip scrolls** (audit DSC-7, G1-9). A horizontal `default` TabsList goes through the same ScrollArea and Scroller as a line strip: in a container narrower than its tabs it scrolls from its first tab, with arrows for a pointer, instead of spilling past both edges. See Components/Tabs Responsive Widths.
- **The filled strip's selected chip is outlined** (audit G6-3). The indicator adds an inset `color.border.selected` outline, 3:1 against the track on every surface in both modes, since its fill is about 1.1:1 against the track and darker than it in dark mode; contrast.test.mjs holds the pair. See Components/Tabs Variants.
- **A click between two line tabs chooses the nearer** (audit DSC-16, the Tabs half). A horizontal line tab's hit area reaches half way across the gap to its neighbour; labels, underline and the strip's scroll width are unchanged. See Components/Tabs Responsive Widths.
- **Tabs reveal focus through the Scroller** (audit DSC-12). TabsList's own reveal is gone; the Scroller's handles right-to-left strips, scroll padding and tabs wider than the strip. See Components/Tabs Responsive Widths and Orientation.
- **ScrollArea's ring and thumb** (audit NAV-4, NAV-13, TOO-1's scroll-area site). The viewport's focus ring is drawn inside it on the root's corners (radius from scroller.css, not an arbitrary class), and in forced colours the thumb is `CanvasText`, `Highlight` under the pointer. See Components/ScrollArea Vertical.
- **ScrollArea, Scroller and Tabs pages** (audit NAV-15, DOC-8, DSC-13). ScrollArea's page says when to use it, how to name it and shows the ScrollArea and ScrollBar props; Scroller's page documents focus, the tab stop, long lists and growth; Tabs points counts at Count with one `max` per product. See Components/ScrollArea, Components/Scroller and Components/Tabs.

- **Loading keeps a button's width, and can say so** (audit BTN-7, BTN-6, FDB-14). `isLoading` no longer inserts a spinner before the label: the spinner takes the leading icon's place, else the trailing icon's, else it sits over the label, which keeps its space and stays the accessible name, so a footer never shifts on save. The spinner takes the text colour, which also keeps it visible in forced colours. The new `loadingLabel` says once, through the page's polite live region, what started ("Saving requirement revision"), since `aria-busy` alone is silent. See Components/Button Loading keeps its width and says so.
- **IconButton and LinkIconButton take `xsmall` and `xxsmall`** (audit BTN-12, BTN-13). `xsmall` is 24px, beside an xsmall Button; `xxsmall` is the 20px row control (a table or tree row's disclosure and row actions, a list row's chevron) with the small radius, a 14px icon and a 24px hit area where any pointer is coarse, so a row no longer hand-rolls one or shrinks an IconButton with a class. The Button and IconButton pages record the family's one height axis and the pairing with Toggle's shadcn size names, and that `size` is ignored on `variant="link"`. See Components/IconButton Matrix and In a dense row, and Components/LinkButton LinkIconButton.
- **Button checks Label in Name** (audit BTN-5, the Button half). A Button whose `aria-label` leaves out its visible text says so once on the console ("Ledger: …"), since a speech-input user says the words they see (WCAG 2.5.3). The page's Content section says to keep the visible words as the name and add context with `aria-describedby`. See Components/Button Don't.
- **Button's page chooses, ranks and words** (audit BTN-9, BTN-19). New sections: Choosing between them (Button, IconButton, LinkButton, TextLink, Toggle, ToggleGroup, CopyButton), Hierarchy (one primary per region), Content (verb and object, never OK or Submit alone, keep the label while loading) and Don't. Toggle and ToggleGroup gain a choosing table; the shipped migration notes leave the Button, Toggle, ToggleGroup and ButtonGroup pages (they stay in the 0.6.0 and 0.5.0 entries below). See Components/Button Don't.
- **ButtonGroupText takes its neighbours' size** (audit BTN-18). `size` is `xsmall`, `small` or `medium` (the default): the text is never shorter than the buttons beside it, so a vertical group's text keeps their height, and its content is centred. See Components/ButtonGroup Orientations and Composition.
- **A single-select ToggleGroup's tab stop is its pressed item** (audit G7-10). As in a radio group, Tab lands on the current choice, not the first item; arrow keys still reach every item, Tab from any of them leaves the group, and a value changed from outside moves the stop. A `multiple` group, or one whose pressed item is disabled, keeps the first enabled item. The DataTable preset strip and ModeSwitch follow. See Components/ToggleGroup Tab stop on the pressed item.
- **ModeSwitch names its icons, reads storage as a store and hears other tabs** (audit BTN-14, TOK-11, TOK-16). Icons only, each item's name shows in a tooltip on hover and keyboard focus. The locale defaults match the page: `colorMode` is "Appearance" (was "Colour mode") and `systemMode` "System" (was "Match system"). ModeProvider reads the stored mode through `useSyncExternalStore`, so the reader's choice is pressed from the first client render (the server renders System), a provider with the same key hears another on the page, a choice made in another tab arrives through the `storage` event, and a choice storage refuses lives for the page. See Components/Mode Stored, and shared across tabs.
- **Forced colours keep a bounded button's edge** (audit BTN-8). Primary, secondary and danger Buttons, IconButtons, LinkButtons and LinkIconButtons carry `data-button-variant`, and forced-colors.css gives those three variants a ButtonText outline inside their box (GrayText when disabled), since forced colours remove the fill and the raised shadow that drew it. See Components/Button Matrix in the forced-colours project.
- **Escape leaves an unavailable button** (audit FLT-4, PRV-7, the button half). Base UI's focusable disabled button prevents the default of every key but Tab, and a Shell.Panel and the side nav overlay leave an Escape whose default is prevented alone, so Escape on a loading Button, one with `disabledReason` or one with `focusableWhenDisabled` never closed the surface around it. The button's Base UI key handlers now skip Escape; Enter and Space stay blocked, and an open tooltip still takes the first Escape. See Components/IconButton Disabled but reachable.
- **ButtonGroupSeparator draws its rule** (audit TOK-2, the kit half). Its orientation classes were keyed on `data-horizontal` and `data-vertical`, which Base UI never sets, so the group's rule that removes every later child's start edge left the separator with no line at all; they are keyed on `data-orientation`, and a split action (Export beside its options, the program's Create system) shows its 1px divider. See Components/ButtonGroup Orientations and Composition.
- **A disabled IconButton that stays reachable** (audit FLT-4, PRV-7, the button half). The IconButton page says when to keep an unavailable icon-only control in the tab order with `focusableWhenDisabled`: its tooltip, its only visible label, still shows on hover and focus, and one that becomes disabled while focused keeps focus. See Components/IconButton Disabled but reachable.

- **Facets that hold** (audit DTP-6, DTP-7). A DataTable facet keeps its order (a status map's, else the alphabet's, never the counts'), keeps a chosen value listed at 0 when the search or another filter removes its rows, says "No values to filter by" when it is empty, takes a search field above eight values, and scrolls inside a popover capped at the window. A column whose `getUniqueValues` gives each row's values (a `c.list` column given one, say) facets each value once, counted per row; its `filterFn` then matches a row that holds any value chosen. See Patterns/Data table Facets that hold and Filters follow live rows.
- **Date and number ranges in the reader's words** (audit DTP-14, DAT-9). A date filter's ends are two DatePickers, each limiting the other; the chip reads "Mar 1 – May 31, 2026" instead of ISO text, a number's placeholders use the locale's numerals, and a start after the end is flagged at the end with `aria-invalid` and a line that says so. See Patterns/Data table Date and number ranges.
- **Server facets and counts** (audit DTC-10). `DataTable.Filter` takes `options` (`FilterOption`: `value`, `label`, `count`), `DataTable.Filters` takes them by column, and `DataTable.Presets` takes `counts` by preset id, for a table the server filters, whose rows are one page. See Patterns/Data table Server facets and counts.
- **The saved-question menu is named by what it shows** (audit DTP-5, TLB-3, BTN-5, DTP-18). `DataTable.Presets variant="menu"` no longer puts `aria-label` over its visible words: its name is the question and its count ("All findings 150"), and `aria-label` (the locale's "Saved questions" unsaid) is its description and names the menu's radio group. The count is whole, not 99+, in the locale's numerals, in the trigger, the menu and the strip. A question is in force whenever the table's filters ask it: a facet's values in any order, one value against a list of one. See Patterns/Data table Saved question menu.
- **DataTable.Search is a SearchField** (audit INP-7). The table's own search is the kit's search field at the small size: a searchbox, a clear button while there is a query, Escape to clear, Enter never submits a form around it. See Patterns/Data table Filters follow live rows.
- **Paging keeps focus** (audit DTP-10, TLB-5, API-18). The DataTable pager's Previous and Next are IconButtons that stay focusable at the first and last page (`aria-disabled`), so pressing Next to the end leaves focus on Next; every page is a subtle button and the current one takes PaginationItem's `aria-current` mark. See Patterns/Data table Paging keeps focus.
- **A drag says the place, and only a table that can reorder mounts one** (audit DTP-12, G8-2, A11-17). A column or row drag says where it would land ("Finding moved to position 3 of 3."). DragContext mounts dnd-kit only for a `reorderable` or `reorderRows` table, so other tables have no drag instructions or live region. Headers and rows slide on `motion.duration.medium` and the standard curve instead of dnd-kit's 200ms, and under reduced motion they move at once, with no slide after the drop and no smooth keyboard scrolling. See Patterns/Data table Drag says the place.
- **One active look for the toolbar triggers** (audit DTP-17). Metrics reads Base UI's `data-panel-open`, so an open Metrics takes the selected look Group by and Filters take, from one shared class, instead of the pressed grey. See Patterns/Data table Metrics.
- **The table's menus size to their words** (audit MNU-8, MNU-16). Columns, Settings, the column menu and the saved-question menu set a minimum width, not a fixed one, and the column menu's sort choices are a named group apart from its actions. The column menu's trigger is IconButton's `xxsmall` row control rather than a small one shrunk by a class (audit BTN-12). See Patterns/Data table Moving columns.

- **Toolbar takes a div's props and a ref** (audit TLB-14). `ref`, `id`, `data-` attributes and a role with a name reach the row, which keeps its own `data-slot`. `defaultSearch` lets the toolbar own its query when `onSearch` comes without `search`, so what the reader types shows; the Playground's search is live. A caller that starts or stops passing `search` gets a new field, not an input that switches between the two. See Patterns/Toolbar Native attributes and Playground.
- **A folded filter still says it applies** (audit TLB-6). `activeFilters`, the number of filters that apply (with a DataTable, `table.state.columnFilters.length`), shows on More as a Count while the filters are folded, and More is named "More filters, 2 applied" (locale messages `moreFiltersApplied` and `moreFiltersAndDisplayApplied`). The Constrained story passes it, so Gaps chosen inside More reads "More filters and display options, 1 applied". See Patterns/Toolbar Applied filters, folded and Constrained.
- **What More held is measured as it closes** (audit TLB-15). A filter or display control whose label changed inside More is measured there, laid out as the row would hold it, before the row decides whether to take it back, so a filter that shrank returns to the row when it fits instead of staying folded on a stale width. See Patterns/Toolbar Applied filters, folded.
- **Toolbar's page says where each control goes** (audit TLB-4, API-11, PA3-6, PA4-6, PA2-7). A DataTable announces the result; over a plain Table the caller's count at the end is a polite status (the Live story now has one and a play). A yes-or-no about the rows is a FilterChip toggle in `filters`, and a Select that chooses which rows there are (a revision, an edition, a scope) goes in `views`, where it never folds. The DataTable parts listed are the ones a toolbar holds, the toolbar's own search in place of `DataTable.Search`, and a SelectTrigger in the row is `size="small"`. See Patterns/Toolbar Live.
- **A focused saved view keeps its ring** (audit TLB-2, the Toolbar half). A play tabs from the search to a saved view and checks its whole focus ring sits inside the views strip, which the Scroller's inset rings now allow. See Patterns/Toolbar A focused saved view.
- **Pagination's current page is marked by its state** (audit G6-4). PaginationItem marks the link or button inside it that has `aria-current="page"`: a semibold figure in the default text colour over a 2px bar in `color.background.selected.bold`, 3:1 on every surface in every mode, instead of a raised fill at 1.04:1 in dark; every page is otherwise subtle, and forced colours keep the Highlight fill without the bar. The contrast gate holds the bar on each surface and on the hover tint. See Components/Pagination Links.
- **Pagination keeps its identity and says its words** (audit TLB-17). Every part spreads the caller's props before its own `data-slot`, and Pagination no longer repeats the nav's own role. Previous and Next with `text` are named by it ("Older", not "Next page"), and the ellipsis says "More pages" (`morePages`) to a screen reader. The in-memory pager's arrows are recommended `focusableWhenDisabled`. See Components/Pagination Words of your own, Native attributes and In Memory Table.
- **ActionBar has a page, and a blocked action is reachable** (audit TLB-16). A blocked action stays a Tab stop (`aria-disabled`) and its reason under the row is its accessible description. Its page says that no product screen uses it and that a record page's header is PageHeader. See Patterns/ActionBar A blocked action.

- **Lint gates for the audit's repeated mistakes** (audit MOD-1, A11-4, BTN-1, TOO-4, FDB-1, API-1, G2-14, TOO-1, SEL-9, TOO-14, TOO-15). Six rules, errors in `recommended` and, except the alert rule, in the kit's own preset: `no-overlay-autofocus` (autoFocus inside Dialog, Sheet, Popover, PickerSheet or RecordBrowser content, or in a component of the same file rendered there), `no-disabled-while-loading` (a `disabled` that reuses the `isLoading` flag), `overlay-width-preset` (a style width or a width class on DialogContent, SheetContent, AlertDialogContent or DrawerContent), `no-plain-alert-role` (`role="alert"` on a plain element), `link-button-navigation` (a Button or IconButton that renders a link or only navigates from `onClick`; LinkButton and LinkIconButton instead) and `no-style-design-value` (a literal length, colour, weight or margin in `style`). The token rules now read `classes()`, className callbacks, single-definition consts, `*ClassName` props, `classNames` slot maps and module-level class constants, and `settings.ledger.classFunctions` names a consumer's own helpers. `no-kit-shadow` sees `memo`, `forwardRef` and default exports; the footer-order rule covers AlertDialog, Sheet and Drawer footers and close parts rendered as buttons; a TextLink with no destination is reported; retired hooks are reported at their import. Every rule takes `allow: { "<file>": n }`: a file keeps that many reports, fails above them, and is asked to lower the number below them, so the kit's existing sites (test/lint-allow.json) can only shrink. `plugin.meta.version` is the package's. The stale LEGACY self-maps and the root config's shell.tsx exemption are gone. See Guidance/Lint rules.
- **Story gates** (audit A11-12, TOO-7, TOO-8, TOO-9, TOO-17, G1-12, G3, G5, STR-9). `storybook-light` measures every tab stop's focus ring and fails a story where a clipping ancestor cuts half of it; `storybook-forced-colors` runs every family and fails a selected, pressed, current or checked item that looks like its unselected sibling; `npm run test:gates` adds `storybook-touch` (a 390px phone with touch, a coarse pointer and no hover: axe's target-size rule, counting the touch-target hit areas, and no hover-only actions), `storybook-short` (overlay families at 320 by 256: every control at least half in view when focused, the title reachable) and `storybook-long` (titles, values and stamps lengthened by 40% with an unbroken 80-character token, in the 320px frame). Every project also fails on Base UI and React warnings on `console.warn`. test/story-gates.ts measures, and test/gates-allow.json holds the stories that predated a gate, the plays a gate project skips and why; `LEDGER_GATES_RECORD=1` writes each story's count instead of failing. See Guidance/Testing and review.

- **Choice-control boundaries at 3:1** (audit A11-3, TOK-3, CTL-7, CTL-3; D2). Checkbox and Radio draw their box in `color.border.bold`, 3:1 against every surface (the field border was 1.87:1); text fields keep `color.border.input`, which increased contrast raises. The Switch's off track is a new token, `color.background.input.track` (`neutral.600`, `darkNeutral.600` in dark), 3:1 against every surface with the thumb 3:1 on it, in place of `color.background.neutral` (1.12:1), which buttons and badges keep. contrast.test adds the pairs in every mode. See Components/Checkbox Boundary, Components/RadioGroup Boundary and Components/Switch Boundary.
- **A second cue for highlight, pressed and selected** (audit A11-2, MNU-1, TOK-1, TOK-7, BTN-2, SEL-2, G6-8). The keyboard-highlighted row of a DropdownMenu, Select, Combobox or SearchDialog draws the inset focus outline over its tint (on `:focus-visible` where the list focuses its rows, on the highlight where the row is the active descendant), through `menuItemHighlighted`; the row under the pointer keeps the tint alone. A pressed Toggle or ToggleGroup item takes the selected palette with a 1px `color.border.selected` edge drawn out of the flow, so pressed no longer matches hover and pressing never resizes the item; the outline variant recolours its border. A selected Tree row adds a 2px bar at its start edge in the selected colour. contrast.test holds the edge and the bar at 3:1 on the selected fill and the outline on the highlight tint. See Components/Toggle Pressed against hover, Components/ToggleGroup Toggle Group Matrix, Components/Tree Selected and right to left and Components/DropdownMenu Preferences (open it from the keyboard).
- **Forced colours keep state** (audit A11-2, MNU-1, SEL-2, TOK-1, TOK-4, BTN-8, TOO-7, SHA-5). forced-colors.css maps a highlighted or submenu-open floating-list row (DropdownMenu, Select, Combobox, the cmdk command list, SearchDialog), a pressed Toggle or ToggleGroup item, anything with `aria-pressed="true"` (Button `isSelected`, the preview eye), the selected Tree row, the side nav's current page and Pagination's current page (Pagination and DataTable's page buttons, whose raised shadow forced colours remove) to Highlight with HighlightText inside; a disabled row is GrayText, with a Highlight outline while the keyboard is on it. The forced-colours test project now runs every family (see Story gates); see Components/Tree Selected and right to left and Components/Toggle Pressed against hover, whose plays check the forced-colours branch.
- **TextLink is underlined in a sentence** (audit A11-10). A link whose parent has text of its own beside it (`data-in-text`) is underlined at rest, since its colour against the words beside it is 2.0:1 in dark mode; a standalone link still underlines on hover. See Components/TextLink In Prose.
- **Shell.Panel's focus contract at every width** (audit A11-8, SHB-1, SHB-2). Opening the panel moves focus into it while focus is on the page, the opener or nowhere, at desktop widths as well as where it covers Main; `initialFocus` names another element, or `false` leaves focus in place beside a visible Main. Closing with focus inside returns it to the control the reader last used in Main (another row's eye pressed while the panel was open), not the one captured when it mounted; `finalFocus` overrides it. Escape from a field inside the panel (a text input, a textarea, a select, an editable region) no longer closes it. `ShellPanelFocusTarget` is the new type. See Layout/Shell Panel focus.
- **Right to left in Table and Tree** (audit A11-11, partly). Table and its list line align with `text-start` instead of `text-left`, and the closed chevrons of Table.Group, Table.Tree, Table.Disclosure, a list line and Tree point left in a right-to-left table or tree. See Components/Tree Selected and right to left.
- **Tree.Item `isExpanded`** (audit API-13). The branch's open state is `isExpanded`, beside `isSelected`; `expanded` still works for one version and is `@deprecated`. See Components/Tree Families.
- **Type and control sizes in rem** (audit A11-13). Every `font.*` size and line height, `dimension.control.*` and `dimension.row.*` are in rem (13px is `0.8125rem`), so a reader who raises the browser's default text size gets larger text, controls and rows; at the default size nothing moves. The DTCG exports carry the rem values. See Tokens/Typography Specimen and Tokens/Metrics Dimension.

- **A tone alone is a status** (audit API-2). A Badge given a `tone` and no `variant` is the `secondary` treatment, the subtle fill in that tone, so `<Badge tone="warning">` no longer renders a loud bold pill; `badgeVariants` resolves the same way. Bold is asked for, with `appearance="bold"` or an explicit `variant="default"`; a Badge with neither prop is still the brand bold fill. See Components/Badge Tone without a variant.
- **SelectTrigger takes the control scale** (audit API-5, DOC-12). `size` is `small` (28px) or `medium` (32px, the default), the words Input, Combobox and Button use, and `data-size` reports that word, so InputGroup's rule keyed on `small` matches a Select. `sm` and `default` stay as deprecated spellings for one version and render as before. `SelectTriggerSize` is exported. api/axis-policy.json records every family's size values in one table (`axes.size.familyValues`). See Components/Select Choices.
- **DropdownMenuItem says `danger`** (audit API-6). An action that destroys or cannot be undone is `variant="danger"`, the word Button and RowAction use, so a Delete button and the Delete item beside it agree; `data-variant` reports `danger`. `destructive` is its deprecated spelling for one version and renders the same. Badge keeps its shadcn `destructive` treatment, a different concept, as axis-policy records. `DropdownMenuItemVariant` is exported. See Components/DropdownMenu Descriptions and disabled reasons.
- **Parts by name for their props tables** (audit API-8). `InspectorGroup`, `ItemGroup`, `TimelineItem`, `TimelineGroup` and the PageHeader parts (`PageHeaderLead`, `PageHeaderHeading`, `PageHeaderTitle`, `PageHeaderDescription`, `PageHeaderActions`) are exported by name beside their compounds, the same components. The Attachment, Inspector, Item, PageHeader, Tabs and Timeline pages show each part's props, and their stories declare the parts as subcomponents, so the MCP docs list them. See Patterns/Inspector, Components/Item, Components/Timeline, Components/Tabs, Components/Attachment and Layout/PageHeader.
- **Native props, refs and a part's own `data-slot`** (audit API-9, API-17). Timeline, Timeline.Item and Timeline.Group take their element's native props, a `className` and a ref; so do Count, Dot and Indicator (their refs now reach the span or svg), Inspector and Inspector.Group, and Chart.Frame keeps its figure props. Each part now puts its `data-slot` after the caller's props, so a stray attribute cannot rename it: Attachment's parts, Select's and DropdownMenu's parts, Tabs' parts, Item (`item`), Item.Group (`item-group`), Timeline (`timeline`, `timeline-group`, `timeline-item`), Count (`count`), Dot (`dot`), Indicator (`indicator`), Inspector (`inspector`, `inspector-group`) and Chart.Frame (`chart-frame`, with `data-state`). Count's `max` and `appearance` take `undefined` under `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`. See the Native attributes stories on Components/Timeline, Count, Indicator and Attachment, and Patterns/Inspector.
- **Item.Group names its list** (audit API-16). `aria-labelledby` and `aria-label` reach the `ol` that holds the rows, not the wrapper `div`, which has no role; the group's own `title` still wins. `labelledBy` is the deprecated name of `aria-labelledby` for one version. See Components/Item Group named from outside.
- **Chart.Frame's `state`** (audit API-12). The plot's state is `state`, the word DataTable uses (`ready`, `loading`, `refreshing`, `empty`, `error`); `status` is its deprecated name for one version and `state` wins when both are set. `ChartFrameState` is exported. `status` stays the name of the badge slot on PreviewSheet, Related.Card, TaskRow and Glance. See Patterns/Chart/Overview States.
- **Inspector's page has a Don't** (audit API-10, DOC-5). The page shows the group's props by name, the native attributes and a Do/Don't beside a hand-built rail. See Patterns/Inspector Native attributes and Don't.
- **The Pages register is a Toolbar** (audit API-11). Layout/Pages' register tab starts with a Toolbar (search first, the status filter, Columns, and Create risk at the end) and a `responsive` table, with no Section around the only table, as the product contract asks; its Queue example follows the panel's rule that Escape in a field belongs to the field. See Layout/Pages Register and Queue With Panel.
- **axis-policy matches the exports.** Notes for parts that no longer exist (Block, IndexPage, Panel, PreviewRail, PreviewSplit, ShowPage, the Accordion members) are gone, review sources point at the files that exist, and `npm run ds:api:matrix` runs again. New notes cover Badge's tone resolution, SelectTrigger, DropdownMenuItem, Dot, Item.Group, Timeline's parts, Chart.Frame and TabsList.

- **A Locale page** (audit DOC-6). Components/Locale documents LedgerProvider, which had no page: where it mounts, its four options and what each decides without it (en-US, UTC, ltr, the English messages), nesting, days against instants, the same zone on the server and the client with a `useSyncExternalStore` hand-over after hydration, the `useLedgerLocale()` formatters and `createLedgerLocale`, and the messages. MCP `docs-show` now returns LedgerProvider's props. Getting started gains the root setup (ModeProvider, LedgerProvider with the reader's zone, Toaster, and what Shell mounts), the README's provider example passes the reader's zone instead of `timeZone="UTC"`, and Choosing a part has a locale row. See Components/Locale: The reader's zone, Instants and days, Formatters, Messages, Right to left.
- **The guidance matches the kit** (audit API-19, DOC-8, DOC-9, DOC-10, DOC-11, DOC-14, DOC-15, DOC-16, DOC-17, DOC-18, PRM-16, TOO-13, NAV-15). Lint rules is the one table of every rule, what it reports and which preset turns it on, including `no-native-confirm`, `text-link-navigation`, `dialog-footer-order` and the two opt-in product rules; `use-primitives` is off in `package`, not an error; the component library guide links to it instead of keeping a second table. Token grammar and Which token read the tokens: `radius.medium` is `--ds-radius-medium`, a row is 40 (36 compact), a space name counts hundredths of 8px, `heading.large` is semibold too, Box takes `backgroundColor`, and radius uses are the Shape sheet's one table. Choosing a part says a DataTable or a Table by the task, not the row count, sends a summary of linked records to Related and records the reader works through to an embedded DataTable, and adds Scroller beside ScrollArea. Coming from shadcn lists the new parts and Slider and Input OTP as not in Ledger. The Introduction maps Guidance, Layout and Patterns; Getting started states the export rule and renames its `Empty` example; Writing stories names Storybook 10, puts the usage story first and binds `_lib` helpers locally; the component library guide drops the application Storybook, `src/features/` and `useRecordForm`, and fixes the Pages link. See Guidance/Lint rules, Guidance/Choosing a part and Guidance/Getting started.
- **PreviewNavigation's page matches the preview contract** (audit DOC-7). The outer bar is navigation only, the record's name is the inner PageHeader's h2, the in-panel composition is shown, and focus at an endpoint moves to the remaining enabled control. See Patterns/PreviewNavigation.
- **llms.txt lists every export** (audit DOC-13). The Public exports section is built from `api/public-api.json`, values and types grouped by layer, with the re-exported TanStack types and the `cn` subpath, instead of the lint's component inventory (which missed `defineColumns`, `useDataTable`, `buttonVariants`, `toast` and `cn`). Guidance follows in reading order: Getting started, Choosing a part, Recipes, Which token, then the rest. A page's code examples now keep their import lines (only the page's own imports are dropped), so a copied example names what it imports from `@ledger/design-system`. See `llms.txt`, Public exports and Guidance/Getting started.
- **PickerSheet owns its search, its pending save and its failure** (audit PIK-4, PIK-5, PIK-6, API-7, API-14, G5-5). `table` takes the DataTable the reader chooses from: the search field drives its global filter, so a search with no match shows the table's filtered empty with Clear filters instead of the empty for a collection with nothing to add, and `selected`, `total` and `onClear` come from its selection and from the rows search and filters keep that can be chosen (nested rows counted whether open or not, group bands left out, a server-filtered table's `rowCount`) unless given (all three are now optional). `search` is `{ placeholder }` with a table, or `{ value, onChange, placeholder }` without, as before, and is a SearchField, so Escape empties a query before it closes the sheet. `pending` holds the sheet with Sheet's lock (Escape, the blanket, the close button and Cancel, now a SheetClose, do nothing and `onClose` is not called), disables Back and Clear while keeping focus on them, makes the toolbar and the rows inert and shows the primary's `isLoading` (`action.isLoading` wins). `error` shows what went wrong as a danger Alert above the footer's buttons, outside the scroll. `initialFocus` is forwarded to SheetContent. `onClose` receives Base UI's change details as an optional argument (`details.reason`: `escape-key`, `outside-press`, `close-press`); callers with `() => void` are unchanged. `summary` replaces the count, for a single choice's name. `width` takes a Sheet step, `large` unsaid (760px, as before); a number still works. The count, Back, Search, Clear and Cancel come from the locale, with the new plural messages `pickerNothingChosen`, `pickerToChooseFromOne`/`Other`, `pickerChosenOne`/`Other` and `pickerChosenOfOne`/`Other`, and the footer wraps its count above the buttons when the row is too narrow. New types `PickerSheetCloseDetails` and `PickerSheetSearch`; `PickerSheetProps` takes the row type. See Patterns/PickerSheet: Picker sheet, Search With No Match, Pending And Failure and Controlled Search.
- **The overlay close buttons are IconButtons** (audit API-18). DialogContent's and SheetContent's close button and PickerSheet's Back are IconButtons with `isTooltipDisabled`, so they share IconButton's size, touch target and pending behaviour instead of a hand-built Button with an `aria-label`; their names, places and `data-slot` are unchanged. See Components/Dialog: Form; Components/Sheet: Pending; Patterns/PickerSheet: Picker sheet.
- **The overlay pages list Base UI's props** (audit DOC-1). Dialog, Sheet, AlertDialog and Drawer name the root and popup props they take from Base UI (`open`, `defaultOpen`, `onOpenChange` and its details, `onOpenChangeComplete`, `modal`, `disablePointerDismissal`, `actionsRef`, `handle`, `initialFocus`, `finalFocus`, and the Drawer's snap points) beside Ledger's own, link Base UI's API reference, and add ArgTypes for their Trigger parts. See Components/Dialog, Components/Sheet, Components/AlertDialog and Components/Drawer, Props.

- **Guidance follows the new parts.** Getting started and Coming from shadcn send button-styled navigation to LinkButton instead of `buttonVariants` on a router link; the CodeBlock page links a comparison to Diff; the README lists `fonts.css` and the two increased-contrast DTCG documents. The Bound in a Field plays on RadioGroup and Switch check the token colours only outside forced colours, where the system colours replace them and the focus ring is forced-colors.css's Highlight outline. The packed-consumer smoke imports `fonts.css` and checks that the Geist faces reach the consumer's CSS, and its KeyValue check expects Truncate's reveal where the native `title` used to be. See Guidance/Getting started, Components/RadioGroup Bound in a Field and Components/Switch Bound in a Field.
- **Choosing a part covers the audit's new parts.** Rows for Field binding, ErrorSummary, CheckboxGroup, SearchField, NumberField, the date fields and displays, FileTrigger and DropZone, LinkButton, `disabledReason`, CopyButton, the downloads, Truncate, Icon, VisuallyHidden, List, Prose Markdown, Diff, KbdShortcut, the chart table twins, collapsible Sections, HeadingLevelProvider, the Shell's page changes and top-nav search, SearchDialog, DataTable.Sort, useTableQuery, StatusMap, the overlay bodies, `pending` and `width`, announce, increased contrast and the packaged fonts; the stale `buttonVariants` link row now points at LinkButton, and the page gains an "Across the product" table. One spelling per idea across the batch: the date fields report through `onValueChange` (DatePicker keeps `onChange`, deprecated for one version), a collapsible Section takes `isCollapsible` as Item does, DateTime takes `isTooltipDisabled` as IconButton does, the Diff fold's messages are `diffShowLinesOne`/`Other` and `diffHideLinesOne`/`Other` like the other plural pairs, the range placeholder key is `chooseDates` beside `chooseDate`, and TimeField's zone slot is `time-field-zone`. See Guidance/Choosing a part.

- **DateTime, RelativeTime, DateLabel** (audit GAP-9, PRT-8, PA5-12, DAT-3; missing.md "DateTime, RelativeTime and DateLabel"). New components: every date the product shows through the LedgerProvider's locale and zone, in a `<time>` with its ISO value. An ISO day never shifts; an instant (ISO with an offset, a Date or epoch ms) shows in the provider's zone; nothing shows Absent. `format` is `date`, `datetime`, `time` or Intl options, `showTimeZone` adds the zone. The full value ("Friday, September 18, 2026 at 5:30 PM Pacific Daylight Time") opens in a tooltip on hover and keyboard focus and is the focused description (`isTooltipDisabled` leaves it out, as on IconButton); a lone date takes a tab stop, one inside a link, button or focusable row opens on its host's focus, and one in a row that cannot take focus (a plain table) keeps its own tab stop. RelativeTime words through Intl.RelativeTimeFormat and follow the clock (a fixed `now` stops them). DateLabel marks overdue, due today and due within `soonWithin` days with an icon and words ("Overdue", "Due today", "Due tomorrow") besides the tone; `complete` shows a finished date plainly. See Components/DateTime: Formats, Full Value, Relative, Updating, Due Dates and Dont.
- **DateRangePicker** (audit DAT-9, DAT-10, GAP-6; missing.md "DateRangePicker"). New component: a range of ISO days, `{ start, end }`, reported through `onValueChange`, from a trigger that reads the range ("Sep 7 – 11, 2026", described in full words) and a popover with two months on Calendar's range mode. The first day starts a new range and the second ends it; the popover then closes and returns focus, and a half-chosen range is never reported ("Choose the end date." shows and is announced meanwhile). `presets` are named ranges beside the months, pressed with a check when they match, and a preset that reaches a day that may not be chosen stays reachable with its reason (Button's `disabledReason`); `min`, `max` and `isDateUnavailable` as on DatePicker; the trigger is the part's `data-slot="date-range-picker"`; `startName` and `endName` for native forms; inside a Field it is the Field's control. See Components/DateRangePicker: Playground, With Presets, Min And Max and Dont.
- **DateTimeField** (audit DAT-4, DAT-5, INP-14, GAP-6; missing.md "TimeField, or a date-and-time field"). New component: a moment, a typed or picked day and a typed time in the provider's zone, whose short name follows the time ("PDT") and whose long name is in the time's accessible name. It holds and reports an ISO instant in UTC through `onValueChange`; a value with no offset (what `datetime-local` holds) is read as a wall time in the provider's zone. A skipped daylight-saving time moves forward, a repeated one takes the earlier moment. `defaultTime` fills the time when a day is picked first; one half left empty says "Enter both a date and a time." once focus leaves; `min` and `max` are instants. Inside a Field the day input is the Field's control and the time input follows its hint, error and state. A native form reset puts back the value, a half entry and its message. See Components/DateTimeField: Playground, In Field, Default Time And Incomplete, Min And Max, Form Reset and Dont.
- **TimeField** (audit DAT-4, GAP-6; missing.md "TimeField"). New component: a typed time of day, held and reported as an ISO time ("17:30") through `onValueChange`. "5pm", "17:30", "1730", "9.15 Uhr" and the locale's day periods and digits all read on Tab or Enter; the field shows the locale's clock, or `hourCycle`. A spinbutton: Up and Down step by `step` minutes (15) on its grid, Page Up and Page Down by an hour, within `min` and `max`, and wrap at midnight without them. Its root is `data-slot="time-field"`, with no unnamed group around the one input. Text that is not a time, or one out of range, keeps the reader's text, reports "" and says what fixes it in a FieldError under the field (`onEntryError` receives it). See Components/TimeField: Playground, Time Field Matrix, Typed Entry, Clocks and Dont.
- **DatePicker** (audit DAT-1, DAT-2, DAT-11, DAT-13, DAT-14, DAT-15). `onValueChange` reports the day, the name the kit's other value controls use; `onChange` still runs and is deprecated for one version. `min`, `max` and `isDateUnavailable(iso)` (true, or the reason as a sentence) disable days with the reason in each day's name, stop the month paging past the limits and, when today may not be chosen, disable Today while keeping it in the tab order with its reason (Button's `disabledReason`). `entry="type"` makes it a text field that reads the day in the provider's locale on Tab or Enter ("Sep 18, 2026", "9/18/2026", "18.9.2026", "2026-09-18", "9/18"), keeps text that is not an allowed day with a FieldError under the field that says what fixes it, reports "" meanwhile (`onEntryError` receives the message), reverts on Escape and opens the month on Alt+Down. `calendarProps` passes `captionLayout`, `startMonth`, `endMonth`, `showWeekNumber` and `fixedWeeks`. Inside a Field the picker is the Field's control, taking its label, hint, error, `invalid` and `disabled`. The picking button is described by the chosen day first (it was left out of what a screen reader heard), and the month is named after its field instead of "Choose a date". A value that is not an ISO day warns once: a timestamp is read as its day in the provider's zone, anything else as empty, so the field never shows one day and submits another. `entry` defaults to `pick`, so existing pickers render as before. See Components/DatePicker: Min Max And Reasons, Today Unavailable, Year Dropdown, Typed Entry, Typed In German and In Field.
- **Calendar** (audit DAT-6, DAT-7, DAT-8, DAT-11). The month follows the LedgerProvider's locale: `lang` on its root, the first day of the week (Monday in de-DE, Saturday in ar-EG; en-US stays Sunday), weekday and grid names, day digits, the year dropdown, week numbers and the navigation's name ("Month navigation"). A caller's own date-fns `locale`, `weekStartsOn` and `lang` still win. Range ends are named "Start of range" and "End of range" instead of "Selected", and a completed range is announced once, politely (a one-day range as its day). `describeDay(date, modifiers)` adds a reason to a day's name. The selected day and the range keep their colours in forced colours. See Components/Calendar: Localized, Calendar Range and Disabled Day Reasons.
- **Locale: days, times and instants** (audit DAT-3, DAT-8, PRT-8). `useLedgerLocale()` gains `formatDay`, `formatTime`, `formatDayRange`, `formatRelative`, `parseDay`, `parseTime`, `zonedParts`, `toInstant`, `timeZoneName`, `hourCycle` and `weekStartsOn`, and caches its date formatters; `CalendarDay` and `WallTime` are exported types. New messages: `datePart`, `timePart`, `dateCalendarNavigation`, `dateInvalid`, `dateTooEarly`, `dateTooLate`, `dateUnavailable`, `dateUnavailableReason`, `dateUnavailableDay`, `dateUnavailableDayReason`, `dateEarliest`, `dateLatest`, `timeInvalid`, `timeTooEarly`, `timeTooLate`, `dateTimeIncomplete`, `chooseDates`, `dateRangeSpoken`, `dateRangeStart`, `dateRangeEnd`, `dateRangeSelected`, `dateRangeChooseEnd`, `dateRangePresets`, `dateOverdue`, `dateDueToday` and `dateDueSoon`. See the stories above; Components/DatePicker: Typed In German and Components/TimeField: Clocks show another locale.

- **SearchField** (audit INP-7, TLB-9; missing.md "SearchField"). New component: one search input with the search icon, `type="search"` and `enterKeyHint="search"`, in the `medium` (32px) and `small` (28px) sizes, controlled (`value`, `onValueChange`) or not (`defaultValue`). A clear button, named by the locale's new `clearSearch` ("Clear search"), shows while there is a query the reader can change; it is not a Tab stop, takes a 24px target under a coarse pointer and keeps focus in the field. Escape clears a query and stops there, so a second Escape reaches the dialog or popover around the field. Enter never submits a surrounding form; `onSubmit` receives the query for a search that runs on Enter, and Enter or Escape that confirms or cancels an input method's composition is left alone. Clearing sends the input event typing does, so `onValueChange`, native `onChange` and a Field's state follow, then `onClear` runs; the clear button also follows a native form reset. Neither the frame nor the icon is an unnamed `role="group"` (INP-16), so a screen reader meets one searchbox. `className` and `style` go to the frame, the ref and the other props to the input; inside a Field it takes the label, hint, error, `invalid`, `required` and `disabled`, and without a label it is named by its placeholder or the locale's `search`. See Components/SearchField: Playground, States, Clear And Escape, Inside A Form, Runs On Enter, Bound in a Field, In A Dialog, Localized, Right To Left and Dont.
- **NumberField** (missing.md "NumberField"). New component on Base UI's NumberField: a number typed or stepped, formatted and parsed with `format` (`Intl.NumberFormat` options: units, currencies, percentages) in the closest LedgerProvider's locale rather than the browser's (`locale` overrides it), clamped to `min` and `max`, stepped by `step`, `largeStep` (Shift) and `smallStep` (Alt) from the arrow keys, with Home and End to the bounds. Decrement and increment buttons are named by the locale's new `decreaseValue` and `increaseValue` and, inside a Field or with `aria-labelledby`, by that label ("Increase Retention"); a field named only by `aria-label` names them through the new `decreaseValueFor` and `increaseValueFor` ("Increase Retention days"); they are not Tab stops, take a 24px target under a coarse pointer and are unavailable at a bound. The input's role description is the locale's new `numberField`. The wheel changes nothing unless `allowWheelScrub`, and then only the focused field. Read-only drops the buttons. `size` is `medium` or `small`; `className`, `style` and `render` go to the root, the ref, `placeholder`, `autoFocus` and the `aria-*` props to the input; a form receives the raw number under the Field's or the field's `name`. Inside a Field it takes the label, hint, error, `invalid`, `required` and `disabled`. See Components/NumberField: Playground, States, Keyboard, Typed And Formatted, Bound in a Field, Wheel, In A Form, Localized, Right To Left and Dont.
- **Toolbar's search is a SearchField** (audit TLB-1, TLB-9). Enter in the search no longer submits a form around the toolbar, so a toolbar inside a dialog's form cannot save it when the reader presses Enter to search; the search has a clear button, and the clear button and Escape call `onSearch("")`. Props, the fold, its measurements and the DOM order are unchanged; the search's frame carries `data-slot="search-field"` in place of `input-group`. See Patterns/Toolbar Inside a form.

- **Increased contrast** (audit TOK-3, TOK-7, G6-7, G6-8; missing.md "Increased-contrast mode"). A second set of colour values for a reader who asks for more contrast, in light and in dark, while the recorded standard values stay the default and render exactly as before. `prefers-contrast: more` turns it on for the document unless the root pins `data-contrast-mode="no-preference"`; `data-contrast-mode="more"` pins it on any element, and the attribute nests with `data-color-mode` either way. Under it the field and choice-control boundary (`color.border.input`) and the hairline (`color.border`) reach 3:1 on every surface, the pressed and highlighted fills (`color.background.neutral` and `.subtle`, rest, hover and pressed) and the selected fills reach 3:1 against the surface they sit on, and `color.text`, `.subtle`, `.subtlest`, `.selected` and `color.icon.subtle`, `.subtlest`, `.selected` step up so text keeps 4.5:1 and icons 3:1 on those fills, and the focus ring (`color.border.focused`) goes darker in light and lighter in dark, so focus on a selected or hovered row keeps 3:1 against the fill as well as the surface. A token source carries it as `$extensions.ledger.contrast` (`light`, `dark`), each naming a palette step; eleven palette steps are new (`neutral.450A`, `.520A`; `darkNeutral.450A`, `.460A`, `.470A`; `blue.50`, `.475`, `.550`, `.675`, `.725`, `.1100`). `contrast.test` runs every pairing in four modes and adds the 3:1 boundary, state and on-fill pairs; the package exports `tokens.dtcg.light-contrast.json` and `tokens.dtcg.dark-contrast.json`; the Storybook toolbar has a Contrast axis. Color.mdx no longer says the standard field border meets 3:1 (TOK-9). See Tokens/Color Increased Contrast and Components/Mode Increased Contrast.
- **Packaged font faces** (audit TOK-6; missing.md "Packaged font faces"). `@ledger/design-system/fonts.css` ships Geist's variable font, upright and italic, from `@fontsource-variable/geist`, now a dependency of the package; `font.family.body` and `.heading` name "Geist Variable" first, and `font.family.code` names "Geist Mono Variable" first, ready for the mono face. The Storybook imports `fonts.css` as a product does. Nothing loads it unless a stylesheet imports it. See Tokens/Typography Packaged Faces.
- **Container-size tokens** (missing.md "Container-size tokens"). `dimension.container.*` is the `--container-*` theme namespace, one token per size: Tailwind's `3xs` to `7xl` restated at their own values, so every `@md:` and `@3xl:` class keeps its width, and the kit's `compact` (25rem) and `split` (50rem). `reset.css` clears Tailwind's copies. RecordBrowser's one-pane switch reads `theme(--container-split)` in place of `800px`. See Tokens/Metrics Container Sizes.

- **Field binds its control** (audit INP-1, INP-13, SEL-3, GAP-1, FDB-1; D1). Field is Base UI's Field.Root, FieldLabel its Field.Label, FieldDescription its Field.Description and FieldError its Field.Error, so inside a Field the label names the control (`htmlFor` and `aria-labelledby`), the hint and the error describe it (`aria-describedby`, in render order), and no id or ARIA is written by hand. New props: `invalid` (external validation: TanStack's `isTouched && !isValid`; a FieldError that shows a message also marks the Field invalid), `disabled` (disables the control, dims the label; a Field nested in a disabled Field, such as each choice of a group, is disabled too), `required` (announced as `aria-required` and marked with the label's asterisk; not a native constraint), `render`, and Base UI's `name`, `validate`, `validationMode`, `validationDebounceTime`, `actionsRef`, `dirty` and `touched`. They reach Input, Textarea, InputGroupInput, the Select trigger, the Combobox input, Checkbox, RadioGroup and Switch; a Select or Combobox list inside a Field is named by the label (SEL-16).
