Explicit ids, `htmlFor`, `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid` still win, and `data-invalid` set by hand is read as `invalid`, so existing fields render as before. Base UI also validates a control's native constraints inside a Field (after Enter in a text field, or on change once the Field is invalid), which a field outside one never did. See Components/Field Playground, Every control, Validation, Native validation and Explicit associations, and each control's Bound in a Field.
- **FieldLabel and FieldLegend `required`** (audit INP-4). The asterisk, hidden from the accessible name, defaults to the Field's `required`; `required={false}` hides it where most of a form is required. See Components/Field Every control.
- **FieldError is not a live region** (audit INP-5). The inline error drops `role="alert"`: focus and the control's description carry it, and a form-level failure belongs in an Alert or an ErrorSummary. `match` shows a message for one Base UI validity state; a FieldError with no children inside a Field shows Base UI's own message when the Field fails. A test that found FieldError by its role finds it by its text or `data-slot="field-error"`. See Components/Field Errors and Native validation.
- **FieldSet `disabled` reaches the choice controls** (audit CTL-1). FieldSet is Base UI's Fieldset.Root and FieldLegend its Fieldset.Legend inside one; `disabled` disables every control inside, including the Checkbox, Radio and Switch roots a native `<fieldset disabled>` leaves live, and an ancestor FieldSet's or the surrounding Field's `disabled` wins. A RadioGroup inside a FieldSet is named by the legend. FieldSet takes `render`. See Components/Field Disabled and Components/Switch Bound in a Field.
- **Invalid choice controls keep the focus ring** (audit CTL-2). An invalid Checkbox or Radio shows the danger border and the focus outline stays the focus colour; an invalid Switch draws its danger outline only while unfocused. A RadioGroup with `aria-invalid` marks every item. See Components/Checkbox Invalid and focused, Components/RadioGroup Bound in a Field and Components/Switch Bound in a Field.
- **Textarea on Field.Control** (audit INP-18, partly). Textarea is a native textarea rendered by Base UI's Field.Control, so a Field binds it as it binds Input; its props, events and ref are the textarea's, as before. See Components/Textarea Bound in a Field.
- **ErrorSummary** (audit INP-2, INP-3, GAP-1; missing.md "ErrorSummary"). New component: every issue with a submitted form in one danger Alert with `role="alert"`, titled "There is a problem" (a heading at the contextual level), each issue a button that moves focus to its control (by id, element or finder; a group or a Checkbox's id focuses the visible control) with its label in view. It focuses itself when it appears and again when `focusKey` changes; `onSelect` opens the step that holds a field first; an issue with neither is listed as text, not a button. `ErrorSummaryIssue` is exported; the title is the locale's `errorSummaryTitle`. The Forms recovery story's hand-built "Review name" buttons are replaced by it. See Components/ErrorSummary Playground, On submit, Field on an earlier step, In a section, No issues and Do and don't, and Patterns/Forms Validation and recovery.
- **CheckboxGroup and CheckboxGroupSelectAll** (missing.md "CheckboxGroup"). New components: Base UI's CheckboxGroup rendered as a FieldSet, which owns the ticked values and is named by its FieldLegend, and a select-all box for a group with `allValues` (ticked, mixed or clear; labelled by the locale's "Select all" unless given children). Inside a Field the group takes the hint and error, and a required Field requires the group, not each box; since no checkbox announces it and a group cannot, the legend adds the locale's `requiredGroup` ("(required)") for assistive technology. The Checkbox page no longer sends readers to `@base-ui/react/checkbox-group`. See Components/CheckboxGroup Playground, Select all label, In a Field, Disabled and Do and don't, and Components/Checkbox Parent.
- **Forms on the binding** (audit INP-6, INP-17). The Forms examples use the bound Field: no ids, `invalid` and `required` on Field, validation on submit then on change, primaries named for the operation. The choice groups use CheckboxGroup and a Field around a FieldSet; the recovery story uses InputGroupInput inside InputGroup and messages that name their field. Forms.mdx no longer names an application helper. See Patterns/Forms Fields, Pickers, Choices, Layout, Required on submit and Validation and recovery.

- **Heading level context** (audit PRM-2, PGL-3, D7; missing.md "Heading level context"). `HeadingLevelProvider` sets the level, 1 to 6, a heading placed inside takes: `level` starts an outline, and without it the provider goes one below the surrounding level (the first gives 2, under the page's h1). `useHeadingLevel()` reads it, `undefined` outside every provider; `HeadingLevel` and `HeadingLevelProviderProps` are exported. A `Heading` without `as` takes the contextual level; `as` still wins and `large`, a displayed number, stays a div. Every kit title reads the same context and keeps its default where nothing sets one, so a page without a provider renders as before: PageHeader.Title h1, Section.Title, EmptyTitle and Shell.Panel.Title h2, CollapsibleHeader, AccordionTrigger, Inspector.Group, Item.Group and Related h3. The type ramp is unchanged. See Primitives/Heading Level from context.
- **PageHeader.Title takes `render`** (audit PGL-1, PRV-9, API-4, A11-16). The Title renders through `useRender`: an h1 by default, the contextual level inside a HeadingLevelProvider, and `render={<h2 />}` or a surface's own title part for another element, with the same classes, so a preview or a sheet no longer copies them onto a raw heading. `PageHeaderTitleProps` is `useRender.ComponentProps<"h1">`. See Layout/PageHeader Title levels.
- **Section levels follow the outline** (audit PGL-3, PRM-2, PA1-8, PA2-6, PA4-10, PA5-6). A Section's title takes the contextual level (an h2 where nothing sets one) and a titled Section gives its content the next level, so a Section inside a Section is an h3; an untitled Section changes nothing, and `Section.Title render` still sets the element. A composed Title registers before paint, so the content takes its level in the same frame. This changes existing outlines where Sections nest: the inner titles move down a level. See Layout/Section Nested.
- **Collapsible Section** (audit PGL-12, REC-6; missing.md "Disclosure header and collapsible Section"). `isCollapsible` on a Section with `title` (the name Item uses) folds its content: the title and its Count become a button inside the heading with a chevron after them that turns while the content is open, the action stays outside the heading, and the content is mounted and hidden until found while closed, so drafts survive and find-in-page opens it. Closed until `defaultOpen`; `open` and `onOpenChange` control it. See Layout/Section Collapsible.
- **CollapsibleHeader** (audit DSC-2, DSC-3, G8-4; missing.md "Disclosure header and collapsible Section"). A disclosure's header: the trigger inside a heading at the contextual level (an h3 where nothing sets one), the title at the start and a chevron at the end that turns on the trigger's `data-panel-open`, the row tint under the pointer, the focus ring on the keyboard, and no movement under reduced motion. Props, the ref and `render` go to the trigger; its children, Counts included, are the title. `CollapsibleHeaderProps` is exported. The Collapsible page documents the state selectors (`data-open` on the Root, `data-panel-open` on the trigger, never Radix's `data-state`). See Components/Collapsible Header and Header levels.
- **Open disclosures stop clipping focus rings** (audit G3, A11-1; missing.md "Inset focus ring utility", Collapsible panels). CollapsibleContent and AccordionContent clip only while they fold: once settled open (Base UI writes the panel height as `auto`) their overflow is visible, so the ring of a link, a button or a table's first cell at the content's edge shows whole. This covers a collapsible Section, Inspector.Group and Inspector's groups. See Layout/Section Collapsible, Components/Collapsible Header, Components/Accordion Heading level and Patterns/Inspector Group.
- **Inspector.Group on the disclosure header** (audit REC-4, REC-6, G2-5, DSC-3, REC-16). The group's title is a CollapsibleHeader, so its chevron now turns when the group opens (it keyed off the Root's `data-open`, which the trigger never has) and its heading takes the contextual level. `defaultOpen` (true by default), `open`, `onOpenChange` and `className` are new; `defaultOpen={false}` is the collapsed Details group. Inspector's data form is made of Inspector.Groups, so its groups take the same level. Inspector has a page of its own. See Patterns/Inspector Groups, Group, In a rail, Controlled and Playground.
- **AccordionTrigger, Item.Group and Related take the contextual level** (audit PRM-2, REC-16, DSC-3). Each heading was a fixed h3; it is now the context's, an h3 where nothing sets one. See Components/Accordion Heading level, Components/Item Group heading level and Patterns/Related Heading level and surface.
- **Shell.Panel starts its outline at 2** (audit PGL-3). The panel is a landmark beside Main, portalled from wherever the route renders it, so it resets the level to 2 for its title whatever surrounds it in React. Panel.Body gives its content the next level when a title names the panel (a Section in a titled panel is an h3) and 2 when none does, as in a preview whose record title is the body's first heading. The panel's focus behaviour is unchanged. See Layout/Section In a titled panel and In a preview panel.
- **EmptyTitle is a heading** (audit EMP-3; missing.md "EmptyTitle heading level"). In the default size the title renders a heading at the contextual level, an h2 where nothing sets one and one below a titled Section, so a page whose content is an Empty keeps it in the outline; in compact it stays body text in a div. `render` sets another element (`<h1 />` for a missing record's page, `<div />` for none), and `EmptyTitleProps` is `useRender.ComponentProps<"h2">`. DataTable's empties inherit it. See Components/Empty Title level.
- **Related.Card records its surface** (missing.md "Overlay surface context", Related.Card's share). A card paints the raised surface and sets it as the current one, as Card does, so a child using `bg-surface-current` matches the card. See Patterns/Related Heading level and surface.

- **DialogBody, SheetBody, DrawerBody and AlertDialogBody** (audit MOD-2, MOD-3, API-3, DOC-2, G5-3; missing.md "DialogBody and SheetBody"). New parts: the one scrolling region between an overlay's fixed header and footer, with the family's inset (Dialog and Drawer `space.250`, Sheet `space.200` by `space.150`, AlertDialog the header's inline inset with no divider), `overscroll-none` (Drawer `overscroll-contain`), at least 80px tall, `ref`, `className`, `render` and `data-slot`. While a body overflows with nothing inside the keyboard can reach, it is a tab stop named by the locale's new `contentScrolls` ("Content, scrolls"). A form goes inside the body with its submit button in the footer through `form`; a fieldset is never the scroller. See Components/Dialog: Scrollable and Long Form; Components/Sheet: Edges And Scrolling; Components/Drawer: Positions; Components/AlertDialog: With Body.
- **Overlays stay operable in a short window** (audit G5-1, G5-3, G5-5, MOD-2). The Dialog, Sheet and AlertDialog popups and the Drawer's content are the fallback scroller (`overflow-y-auto`), so focus can no longer scroll a popup a pointer cannot, and their footers stay at the bottom (`sticky`). Under 30rem of window height a body grows to its content instead of scrolling inside, so the header (and PickerSheet's toolbar) scroll away with it and the footer stays: at 844 by 390 and at 320 by 256 CSS px every field and the primary can be reached and seen, and scrolling up brings the title and Close back. See Components/Dialog: Short Window and Zoomed Window; Patterns/PickerSheet: Short Window; Patterns/RecordBrowser: Short Window.
- **`pending` on Dialog, Sheet and AlertDialog** (audit MOD-4, GAP-4; D8). The root holds the overlay while a command runs: every request to close (Escape, the blanket, the close button, a DialogClose, SheetClose or AlertDialogCancel, focus leaving a non-modal popup) is cancelled before `onOpenChange` hears of it; those close parts are disabled, and the built-in close button, AlertDialogCancel and a DialogClose or SheetClose rendered as a kit Button or IconButton stay focusable with `aria-disabled`, so focus on one is never dropped to the page; the popup is `aria-busy` with `data-pending`. An imperative close through `actionsRef`, and `open={false}`, still close. It does not disable the form: `<FieldSet disabled={pending}>` and the submit button's `isLoading` do. Callers that cancel in `onOpenChange` themselves keep working. See Components/Dialog: Form and Pending And Nested Popup; Components/Sheet: Pending; Components/AlertDialog: Confirmation And Pending.
- **`width` on DialogContent and SheetContent** (audit MOD-10, TOK-10, API-3, DOC-2). A step instead of a pixel `style.maxWidth`: DialogContent `small` 400px, `medium` 520px (the width without the prop), `large` 760px, `xlarge` 960px and `fullscreen` (the window less the 1rem gutter); SheetContent `small` 320px, `medium` 420px (the width without the prop), `large` 760px, `xlarge` 960px and `fullscreen`. Every step narrows to the window, a caller's `style.maxWidth` still wins, and the popup carries `data-width`. New types `DialogWidth` and `SheetWidth`. AlertDialog keeps `size`. See Components/Dialog: Widths; Components/Sheet: Widths.
- **Overlays record their surface** (audit G6-1; missing.md "Overlay surface context"). The Dialog, Sheet, AlertDialog, Drawer, Popover and HoverCard popups set `utility.elevation.surface.current` to the overlay surface, as Card does for the raised one, so sticky table headers, pinned cells, preview eyes and scroll arrows inside them paint the overlay's colour in dark mode instead of the page's. See Components/Dialog: Scrollable; Components/Sheet: Edges And Scrolling; Components/Overlays: Modal; Components/Popover: Task; Components/HoverCard: On An Id; Components/Drawer: Positions.
- **Overlay headings start below the title** (D7; missing.md "Heading level context"). DialogContent, SheetContent, AlertDialogContent and DrawerContent set the heading level to 3 for what they hold, under their h2 title, whatever the level where the overlay was opened: a Section, an ErrorSummary, an EmptyTitle or a Heading in a dialog is an h3, where a Section or an EmptyTitle was an h2. See Components/Dialog: Scrollable and Form; Components/Sheet: Edges And Scrolling.
- **The form dialog recipe** (audit MOD-1, MOD-5; missing.md "Form dialog recipe"). The Dialog Form story is a real form: a controlled root opened from state, `initialFocus` rather than `autoFocus`, the form in DialogBody with the submit button in the footer through `form`, validation on submit with an ErrorSummary and bound Fields, the pending lock, a failed save that keeps the draft, a discard prompt for a dirty draft, and focus back on the opener. Guidance/Recipes "A create or edit form" documents it step by step. The Dialog, AlertDialog and Sheet pages are rewritten in the present tense (when to use, widths, the body, pending, focus return, content); Overlays no longer promises `returnFocusRef` or a Sheet `onBack`, and describes centred placement and the width steps. See Components/Dialog: Form and Form Save Fails.
- **PickerSheet, PreviewSheet and RecordBrowser on the body parts** (audit API-3, G5-1, G5-5). PickerSheet and PreviewSheet scroll in a SheetBody and RecordBrowser in a DialogBody, with the insets they had. RecordBrowser's `context` now opens the results and scrolls with them instead of taking a fixed row above them (`data-record-browser-context`). While `onConfirm` runs, RecordBrowser holds itself with the Dialog's `pending` lock, so the browser is `aria-busy` and its close button says it is unavailable (`aria-disabled`) instead of looking live and doing nothing; see Patterns/RecordBrowser: Pending confirmation and replacement sessions. See Patterns/PickerSheet: Short Window; Patterns/PreviewSheet: Collection Task; Patterns/RecordBrowser: Short Window.

- **Diff** (missing.md "Diff and comparison view"). A new component that compares two texts: line by line with Myers' shortest edit script, in the component with no dependency, then word by word inside a line that was edited rather than rewritten (at least half its characters kept). Removed lines take −, added lines +, a visually hidden "Added:" or "Removed:", and the changed words a struck-through `<del>` or underlined `<ins>`, so no change is shown by colour alone and forced colours keep them all. `layout="inline"` (default) or `"side-by-side"`; side by side falls back to inline wherever the Diff's own width is under 36rem (a container query, not the viewport). Unchanged runs beyond `context` (3) fold behind a Collapsible button that keeps focus and switches Show to Hide, and folded lines are `hidden="until-found"`, so find-in-page opens them. `beforeLabel`, `afterLabel`, `lineNumbers`, `code`, `label` and `render`; long unbroken text wraps at the frame, and the Diff fills the width it is given, in a flex row too. Past 1,000 line edits (500 word edits in a line) it anchors on what each version has once and compares the gaps, so a long text with scattered edits keeps its unchanged lines. Right to left, the gutter and columns mirror, each prose line takes its own direction and `code` lines read left to right. The header summarizes "3 changed, 1 added, 1 removed" or "No differences". `diffText(before, after)` exports the rows and counts. Every word is a locale message (`diff*`). See Components/Diff: Inline, Side By Side, In A Narrow Container, Beside Other Content, States, Blank Lines, Long Unbroken Text, Code, Right To Left, Localized, Counts and Dont.
- **KbdShortcut and the platform modifier** (missing.md "Platform modifier helper", MNU-18). `KbdShortcut keys="Mod+K"` draws a shortcut written once for every platform: ⌘ K on macOS, iOS and iPadOS, Ctrl K elsewhere, modifiers in the platform's order, named keys (arrows, Enter, Escape, Tab, Backspace, Delete) one way each. Its caps are `aria-hidden` and one visually hidden line names the keys ("Command K"), so screen readers no longer hear a glyph's character name. `getModifierKey()` (for key handlers) and `useModifierKey()` (for renders; `"ctrl"` on the server and through hydration, then the browser's answer) read the platform once for the page. `formatShortcut` and `useFormatShortcut` give the text ("⇧⌘P", "Ctrl+Shift+P"), the spoken label and the `aria-keyshortcuts` value, and `shortcutKeys` gives each key's parts. Key names and word caps are locale messages (`key*`). The Command dialogs' footer (CommandKeys, used by CommandPalette and RecordPicker) now draws its keys with KbdShortcut and takes "to move", "to choose" and "to close" from the locale (`keyHint*`); the `choose` override is unchanged. Existing Kbd, KbdGroup and `label` are unchanged. See Components/Kbd: Shortcuts and Platform Modifier.

- **Text parts: Truncate, VisuallyHidden, Icon, List, Prose, KeyValue.Group** (audit G1-6, G1-8, G1-10, FLT-6, CNT-2, CNT-5, CNT-19, CNT-20, PA4-9; missing.md "Overflow-aware truncation", "KeyValue.Group", "Long-form Prose", "VisuallyHidden and an Icon label", "List"). Nothing existing is removed.
  - **Truncate** and **useIsTruncated** (new). Text cut to one, two or three lines (`maxLines`, the name Text uses), with the whole of it in a tooltip only while it is cut: on hover, on keyboard focus of the link or button it sits in, and on keyboard focus of a control inside it. The tooltip is `aria-hidden`, because the full text stays in the DOM; a caller's `title` replaces it; `fullText` sets what it shows; `render` picks the element. One line clips across only, so focus rings and touch areas inside survive. `useIsTruncated(ref, { maxLines })` reports whether an element you truncate yourself is cut and follows resizes and text changes. See Components/Truncate, Reveals On Hover, Reveals On Keyboard Focus, Control Inside, Own Title and Use Is Truncated Hook.
  - **Text** `maxLines` reveals the same way, unless the caller gives a `title`; a one-line clamp on an inline element (`span`, `strong`, `em`, `small`, `label`) makes it a block, so it has a box to cut. New `preserveLineBreaks` keeps authored line breaks (`pre-wrap`). Text is the one primitive that imports a component (Truncate). See Primitives/Text, Max Lines Reveal and Preserve Line Breaks.
  - **KeyValue** reveals a cut label and a cut value of any kind (a code and a title, a TextLink, a Person) through Truncate, in place of the native `title` it set on plain-string values only. **KeyValue.Group** (new, also exported as `KeyValueGroup`) is one `dl` whose KeyValues become its rows, sharing `labelWidth` through context; `layout="auto"` (the default) puts each label over its value when the value would have less than 8rem beside it, `columns` never stacks and `stacked` always does. A KeyValue outside a group renders as before. A KeyValue inside a row's value (a peek's facts) is its own `dl` again. A standalone KeyValue's `dl` gains `data-slot="key-value"`. See Components/KeyValue, Group, Group In A Narrow Panel, Group Layouts, Group Ends At The Value and Cut Value And Label.
  - **Prose** takes several paragraphs: a string child splits at blank lines and keeps its line breaks, `markdown` renders a safe subset (paragraphs, bulleted and numbered lists, emphasis, strong, inline and fenced code, links) to React elements with no HTML injected and unsafe link addresses refused, `renderLink` routes its links, and `size="large"` sets reading text. `label` is optional; a labelled Prose with a single paragraph renders as before. Native props and a ref reach its root. See Components/Typography, Prose Paragraphs, Prose Markdown, Prose Router Links and Prose Large.
  - **Absent** takes an optional `label`: the dash is hidden from assistive technology and the label ("Not recorded") is read in its place. Without it, Absent renders as before. See Components/Typography, Absent Labelled.
  - **VisuallyHidden** (new, a primitive): text for assistive technology alone, as a span or the element `as` names. See Primitives/VisuallyHidden, Uses.
  - **Icon** (new): a Lucide glyph on the icon size and colour tokens; with `label` it is `role="img"` with that name, without one it is `aria-hidden`. See Components/Icon, Named and Rendered.
  - **List** and **List.Item** (new, also exported as `ListItem`): `ul` or `ol` (`ordered`, `start`) on a logical token inset, `spacing` tight or loose. See Components/List, Semantics, In An Alert and Right To Left.

- **DataTable.Sort** (audit DTP-9; missing.md "DataTable.Sort toolbar menu"). The sort as a toolbar menu, for a responsive register whose folded columns have no header to sort by or to show the sort: the column under Sort by, then the direction in words that follow the column (Oldest first, Lowest first, A to Z, or Ascending for a status or a custom sort). The trigger reads "Sort: Due" with the direction's arrow, its name adds the direction ("Sort: Due, Oldest first"), and choices keep the menu open. It writes the table's sorting, so a drawn header keeps its arrow and `aria-sort` and none claims a folded column's sort. `columns` limits and orders what it offers; each set of radios is one group named by its label (Sort by, Direction); native button props and a ref reach the trigger; it folds into a Toolbar's More with Columns and Settings. `DataTableSort` is exported by name. See Patterns/Data table Sort menu, Sort menu at 390px and Sort playground.
- **A column's width from its menu** (audit TBL-10; missing.md "A single-pointer column width option"; WCAG 2.5.7). With `resizable`, the header's column menu adds Wider and Narrower, which step 32px, stop at the column's minimum and maximum and keep the menu open for another press, and Reset width. A polite status says the width ("Finding, 272 pixels wide"). A resizable column now always has a column menu, and a resizable table has the one column status that reorderable tables have. See Patterns/Data table Column width from its menu.
- **useTableQuery and the table query serializer** (audit DTC-5; missing.md "A table query serializer"). The reader's question (search, sort, column filters, page) outlives the register: `useTableQuery(table, { storage: "session" })` keeps it in the tab under `ledger.table.<view>.query`, and `useTableQuery(table, { search, onSearchChange, prefix })` keeps it in the URL through the product's router, so Back after opening a record, Forward and a shared link restore it. The pure helpers `tableQueryToSearch`, `tableQueryFromSearch`, `tableQueryToString`, `tableQueryParamNames`, `sameTableQuery` and `readTableQuery` write and validate the `q`, `sort`, `filters` and `page` parameters; malformed or unknown slices are dropped, and a restored page waits for its rows. A router that commits its search late gives back earlier questions while the reader types; the hook recognises its own echoes and does not apply them. DataTable.mdx and the view store no longer point at a `src/lib/table-state.ts` that does not exist. See Patterns/Data table Question kept for the session, Question in the URL and Question in a URL that commits late.
- **c.status takes a shared status map** (audit STS-1, kit share; missing.md "Domain status binding"). `c.status(key, { statuses })` takes a `StatusMap` of each stored value to `{ label, tone, rank }`: the badge shows the label in the tone, the column sorts by rank with unknown values after the known ones, the search finds a status by its label as well as its stored value, the filter lists the labels in that order, and export writes the labels. `tone` stays supported and wins over the map; `sortBy` wins over the rank. `StatusMap` and `StatusEntry` are exported. The table's global filter is now the kit's `search` filter: TanStack's `includesString`, plus a status map's labels, so a table without a map searches exactly as before; a `globalFilterFn` passed to `useDataTable` still wins. See Patterns/Data table Shared status map.
- **DataTable's row memo holds** (audit PRF-1, DTC-4; missing.md "A render-budget test"). One checkbox redraws one row, and a preview step the two rows it changes: the leading columns, the fitted layout and the tree's key handler are memoized, `onRowClick` is read through a ref so an inline handler costs nothing, and dnd-kit's sensor options and sortable id lists are stable, since a new one redrew every row and header. New `data` or new column definitions still redraw every row. See Patterns/Data table Render budget.
- **A responsive DataTable redraws only when its fit changes** (audit PRF-2, DTP-21). It fits inside its ResizeObserver and redraws only when a column folds or returns or a pin gives way; the identity column is drawn without a width in the fixed table, so the browser gives it the slack and a panel opening, a side-nav toggle or a splitter drag costs no render. A pinned identity, or one under a group heading, keeps its fitted width as before. `fitFrame` joins `fitColumns` in `responsive.ts`. See Patterns/Data table Render budget.
- **A virtualized DataTable keeps its place while hidden.** A row hidden with its page (Main under a phone-width Shell.Panel) measures 0 tall; the row no longer reports that height, so showing the page again no longer reads as rows growing above the fold and scrolls the table about twenty rows down. See Patterns/DataTable Virtualized.
- **One row menu and one glance card per table** (audit PRF-5). Each row carries only its kebab and its id's glance trigger, which open the table's one menu or card with the row's id (Base UI's detached triggers), so a closed menu costs a row nothing. The row menu sizes to its items instead of 200px. See Patterns/Data table Register.
- **`narrowed` on DataTable** (audit EMP-1, DTC-3). The caller says it has narrowed `data` outside the table (a scope toggle, a route or server filter, a picker's own search): with nothing left the toolbar and the header stay and the filtered empty shows, with the caller's `empty.filtered.action` as the way back; the kit's Clear filters shows only while the table's own search or filters are set, and the default hint only then. See Patterns/Data table Narrowed outside the table.
- **DataTable says its result** (audit A11-5, EMP-2). After a search, a filter, a saved question, the caller's narrowing or a page, one polite line through `announce`, half a second after the last change: "8 of 24 findings", "9–16 of 24 findings", "No matching findings". `noun` (`{ one, other }`, "row" and "rows" unsaid; `DataTableNoun`) is what a row is. The first result is not said. While `state` is `loading` the table is `aria-busy`. See Patterns/Data table Result status.
- **`rowLabel` names a row's controls** (audit A11-9, A11-17, DTC-1 opt-in). `useDataTable({ rowLabel })` gives each row's checkbox "Select REQ-001", its kebab "Row actions for REQ-001", its handle "Reorder REQ-001", its detail chevron "Show details for REQ-001" and its More fields the same name; drags say a row by its label (else the tree's label or its id) and a column by its header, never by a raw id. Without it the names are as before. See Patterns/Data table Reordering rows.
- **A header is two tab stops** (audit A11-9). A column's grip and its resize handle are for the pointer: the grip is named for its column ("Reorder Status column"), hidden from assistive technology and out of the tab order, and DataTable no longer makes the handle a tab stop, since the column menu moves (Move left, Move right) and sizes (Wider, Narrower, Reset width) the column by keyboard. A table with `columnMenu: false` has no such menu, so there the grip and the handle keep their keys. `useColumnDrag` takes a third argument, `{ label, pointerOnly }`: the column's name for the grip, and `pointerOnly` when a menu beside it moves the column; without it the grip keeps Space and the arrow keys, as before. See Patterns/Data table Pinned, resizable, reorderable, Keyboard Resize Matrix and Columns without a menu.
- **Move up and Move down** (audit A11-17; WCAG 2.5.7). With `reorderRows`, the row actions menu gains Move up and Move down, one press each, disabled at the ends, and a polite status says where the row stands ("FND-2200, 2 of 6"). A reorderable table takes an actions column, `c.actions(() => [])` when its rows have no actions of their own. See Patterns/Data table Reordering rows.
- **The name link keeps its focus ring** (audit A11-1, G3-2; G3-5 for the glance). A link in a cut cell value, the register's record name included, cuts itself at the value's width with its own ellipsis and draws its ring inside its box (the `outline-field-focused` geometry), so the clip that ends the value never takes the ring; the preview value clips across only. The id's glance trigger does the same. A link drawn as a button (LinkButton, LinkIconButton) keeps its own box and ring. See Patterns/Data table Name link focus.
- **Preset counts are counted once** (audit PRF-9). `DataTable.Presets` counts each saved question once per set of rows and set of questions, not on every keystroke, and the menu variant counts its list only while it is open. The counts read the presets' own filter values, so a number range's open end (`Infinity`) counts as it filters. See Patterns/Data table Register.

- **LinkButton and LinkIconButton** (audit BTN-4, G2-14; missing.md "LinkButton and LinkIconButton"). Navigation that looks like a button: an anchor, or a router Link through `render`, with Button's variants (`primary`, `secondary`, `subtle`, `danger`), sizes, `iconBefore`/`iconAfter` and their insets, and `isFullWidth`. It is never a Base UI Button, so it keeps its href, middle click and open-in-new-tab, and the "expected a native <button>" error no longer applies. LinkIconButton is the icon-only form with a required `label` and a tooltip. Both take `disabledReason` (below): the link loses its href, stays focusable as a disabled link, and a `render` element is not rendered while it is set. See Components/LinkButton's Matrix, Router Link, LinkIconButton, Disabled with a reason and Don't; Components/Button's Navigation and composition now shows a LinkButton.
- **Disabled with a reason** (audit BTN-15, MNU-14; missing.md "A disabled action with a reason"). `disabledReason` on Button, IconButton, LinkButton and LinkIconButton disables the control but keeps it in the tab order with `aria-disabled`; the reason is its accessible description and shows in a tooltip on hover, on keyboard focus and on a tap (a tooltip alone never sees a tap). IconButton's tooltip shows the label, then the reason, and shows the reason even with `isTooltipDisabled`. A subtle or link Button keeps its disabled face under the pointer while focusable. `DropdownMenuItem` takes `description`, a second line under the label that wraps and is the item's description, and `disabledReason`, which disables the item and shows the reason on that line. No existing prop changed. A reason that comes or goes while the control has focus leaves focus on it: the control is rebuilt inside a different tooltip, and it takes focus back in the same commit. A described menu item's typeahead matches its label alone. See Components/Button's and Components/IconButton's Disabled with a reason and A reason that changes while focused, Components/LinkButton's A reason that changes while focused, and Components/DropdownMenu's Descriptions and disabled reasons.
- **A disabled destructive menu item fades** (audit MNU-7). `variant="destructive"` no longer keeps the danger colour and highlight on a disabled item. See Components/DropdownMenu's Descriptions and disabled reasons.
- **CopyButton** (missing.md "CopyButton and Id copy"; audit CNT-15). Copies `text` and says whether it worked: the icon becomes a check or an alert, "Copied" or "Could not copy" shows in a tooltip that opens by itself, and a polite status says it, while the name and width stay put. Labelled by default ("Copy"); with `label` it is icon-only, for an identifier. `onCopied` and `onCopyError` report the result. Each result is a new line in the status region, so a second copy is heard again. CodeBlock's Copy is now this part, on the block's own surface at `space.050` from the corner; with `copy`, the frame is at least `dimension.control.large` tall and the lines keep `space.500` at their end, so a one-line block holds the button and long lines scroll out from under it (audit CNT-4). The CodeBlock gutter is hidden from assistive technology (audit CNT-13). See Components/CopyButton's Labelled, Copy an id, When copying fails and Don't, and Components/CodeBlock's Code and One line, with a Copy.
- **Breadcrumb router links** (audit NAV-2, NAV-9, NAV-10, PGL-10). `BreadcrumbLink` is always an ancestor, so it drops an `aria-current` it is handed: a router that renders through it (TanStack Router's `createLink(BreadcrumbLink)`) no longer marks every ancestor of the current path as the current page. A router Link given through `render` sets the attribute itself; the page documents exact matching for it. The landmark's default name comes from the locale (`breadcrumb`). See Components/Breadcrumb's Router links and Don't.

- **Chart.Frame says things once** (audit CHF-4, CHF-5). The Frame puts its `data`, `x`, `series`, `size`, `height` and formats in context, and a Bar, Line or Area inside takes them when it sets none (`data`, `x` and `series` are optional on the three). Every part tells its Frame what it draws: its series or groups, slices or branches under its own swatch, the formats it draws with, its height and its table. Without `series`, the legend keys what the part draws, and the table twin and the CSV print the plot's records in the plot's format, so a format given only to the part no longer leaves the table raw. The Frame's props still win. Expand redraws the plot at `large` whatever size it was given inline (the Dialog keeps its 860px, now a named constant), a hidden key the legend no longer holds is pruned when the legend toggles, and while the table shows the part stays mounted and draws nothing, so the twin follows new data. A category chart's CSV is byte for byte what it was. The Frame follows the part it holds: when the part changes kind the legend, the table and the CSV change with it, and when the caller replaces it with something that is no part they leave with it; an empty or failed Frame keeps the last legend. See Patterns/Chart/Overview Said Once, Follows Its Part, and Framed, which now asserts the 320px expanded plot.
- **Chart.Frame's Table toggle keeps its name** (audit CHF-2, CHF-12). The toggle is named "Table" whether it is pressed or not, and `aria-pressed` says whether the table shows; it no longer switches its name between "Show as table" and "Show as chart", which a voice command saying "Table" could not reach (WCAG 2.5.3). "Table", "Download CSV" and "Download PNG" come from the locale (`chartTable`, `downloadCsv`, `downloadPng`); `showTable` and `showChart` stay in the dictionary, unused by the Frame. A query by the old names finds nothing now. See Patterns/Chart/Overview Framed.
- **A hidden series no longer dims the rest** (audit CHF-3). A legend click that hides the series under the pointer no longer dims every series left while the pointer stays; only a mouse hover or keyboard focus highlights, so a tap on a phone no longer leaves the plot dimmed; and a hidden line's stroke swatch hollows as a square does. See Patterns/Chart/Overview Framed and Patterns/Chart/Donut Framed.
- **A table view for every chart kind** (audit CHO-4). The Frame's twin is built by the part: a Scatter one row per point (Point, Group, the Frame's `columns`, a column per axis), so its legend groups never become columns; a Donut one row per slice with its value and share of the whole; a Treemap one row per leaf with a column per level; a Heatmap pivoted. The Table toggle and the CSV appear for these kinds in a Frame with no `data`. `Chart.Scatter` takes `zLabel`, the size axis's name in the tooltip, the card and the table, and `Chart.Treemap` takes `levels`, the table's headings. See Patterns/Chart/Scatter As A Table, Donut Framed, Treemap As A Table and Heatmap Sequential; Treemap Drilldown's tab walk now passes the Table toggle.
- **Visible values on a colour-scale Heatmap** (audit CHO-5). In a Frame, a Heatmap on a sequential or diverging scale with `showValues` unsaid gives the header a Values toggle that prints every value on its cell, for keyboard, touch and magnifier readers who cannot hover; the choice is shared with the table view and the expanded Dialog, and `showValues={false}` offers none. The Heatmap now takes the Frame's `format` when it has none, and draws `large` cells when expanded. Five locale messages name the toggle and the twins' headings (`chartValues`, `chartCategory`, `chartGroup`, `chartName`, `chartShare`). See Patterns/Chart/Heatmap Sequential and Status.

- **Announcer** (audit A11-6). `announce(message, { politeness })` speaks through two persistent, visually hidden live regions (`role="log"`, polite and assertive) that `Announcer` renders. Shell mounts one; a product without a Shell mounts one near its root, and with none mounted `announce` creates the regions in the body and speaks once they settle. Each message is a new line, so the same words twice are heard twice; lines leave after seven seconds; on the server it does nothing. Base UI's modal dialogs leave the regions exposed. Nothing else uses it yet. See Components/Announcer's Playground, Politeness, Without an Announcer, In a dialog and Don't.
- **Shell page changes** (audit A11-7, PGL-11, SHA-3). `Shell` takes `locationKey`, the router's location: when it changes, at every width, the shell closes the phone overlay or the desktop flyout, moves focus to Main without scrolling (focus stays on a control in the page that survived the change; focus in a menu or a dialog outside the shell goes on to Main once the popup hands it back to its trigger or drops it), and announces `document.title`, else Main's first h1, or what `getPageTitle` returns. On a phone, choosing a link in the side nav closes the overlay and moves focus to Main even without `locationKey`; `SideNav closeOnNavigate={false}` opts out, and a modified click never closes it. Any other close from inside the overlay, `useSideNav().collapse()` included, returns focus to the toggle instead of dropping it on the body. `SideNavTrigger` gains `"navigation"`. See Layout/Shell's Page change, Page change at 390px, Destination at 390px (no `locationKey`: a plain click, a modified click and a close from product code) and Keep open at 390px.
- **Main's minimum width** (audit SHA-2, SHB-4). The side nav and the panel no longer leave Main at its padding: Main keeps one list column inside its desktop gutters (`dimension.layout.list` plus `space.300` each side, 388px by default). The splitters stop there and report it in `aria-valuemax`; a remembered width that no longer fits is narrowed by the layout, not rewritten, with the panel's column giving way first, down to its 240px minimum. See Layout/Shell's Main minimum, and Persisted Widths, which now asserts the minimum instead of 720 and 720 at 1440px.
- **SearchDialog** (audit MNU-5, MNU-6, MNU-3, MNU-4, PA1-9, SHA-10, SHB-11; missing.md "Global search"). New pattern: the product's search across record types, on Base UI's Autocomplete rendered inline in a Dialog. Rows show the record's `identifier`, its `title` with its `meta` under it (both wrap) and one `badge`, under their `group`; `id` is identity only, never shown or matched. The dialog matches every word of the query against the title, identifier, meta, group and keywords, ignoring case and accents, or with `filter={null}` shows the caller's own results for a controlled `query`. It distinguishes before a query (`initialResults` or the `prompt`), searching (`loading`: listed results stay, and with none it says Searching…, never No results), no results (`noResults`) and a failure (`error` above the results, with Try again from `onRetry`, which returns focus to the field). The field's `aria-activedescendant` follows the highlighted row as the results change; the count, or the no-results sentence, is spoken through the Announcer once the results hold still, and a failure at once, assertively. Choosing a row closes the dialog, then calls `onSelect`. A result with `disabledReason` (or `disabled`) stays listed and reachable by the arrows, is `aria-disabled`, shows the reason under its meta as its description, and cannot be chosen. The highlighted row is outlined in forced colours. Every word is a locale message (`searchDescription`, `searchPlaceholder`, `searching`, `searchNoResults`, `searchNoResultsHint`, `searchFailed`, `searchResultOne`, `searchResultOther`, `keyHintOpen`). See Patterns/SearchDialog's Search dialog, Matching, No results, Unavailable record, Before a query, Remote search (which also checks that No results is never rendered while a search runs), Search failed, At 390px, Playground and Don't.
- **Shell.TopNav.Search** (audit SHB-11, MNU-12, PA1-9; missing.md "Global search"; responsive-2026-09-24 #7). The top nav's way into the SearchDialog: in a top nav at least 48rem wide a field-shaped button that reads its `label` and shows the shortcut's keys, narrower a Search icon button, both one button named by `label` with `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-keyshortcuts`, both calling `onOpen`. `shortcut` ("Mod+K" by default: ⌘K on Apple platforms, Ctrl+K elsewhere; `null` for none) calls `onOpen` from anywhere on the page, and ignores key repeats, other modifiers, text being composed, a key a control already handled (`defaultPrevented`) and a dialog already open. `useCommandPalette` also binds ⌘K, so a page with both gives TopNav.Search another `shortcut` or `null`. Narrowed with focus on the field, focus moves to the icon. The Shell stories' hand-built search helper is gone. See Layout/Shell's Frame, Search at 320px and Search shortcut.
- **Foldable TopNav end items** (audit SHB-9, SHA-11, GAP-10; missing.md "A foldable TopNav end item"). `Shell.TopNav.Item` is an action (`icon`, `label`, `onClick`) or a destination (`href`, or a router link through `render`), with `disabledReason`, drawn as a subtle icon button. In a `TopNav.End` without `overflow`, items fold into one More menu (`moreLabel`, "More" by default) when the top nav is narrower than 48rem, measured on the row itself, so an open panel folds them too: the same icons and names in the row's order, a destination still a link. Every other child of End, such as the ModeSwitch, and an item marked `persistent`, stays in the row; an item that is a menu's or a popover's trigger is `persistent` and takes the trigger through its `render`, since the item forwards its native props and its ref to its button. Narrowed while an item has focus, focus moves to More, never to a persistent item beside it, and back to the first item that left when widened. The top nav is now the size container `topnav` (`container-type: inline-size`): outside a Shell it takes its width from its parent, not from its contents. `overflow` keeps its all-or-nothing behaviour, unchanged; items inside an End with `overflow` never fold. See Layout/Shell's End items fold; Record rail at 390px and Forwarding still cover `overflow`.

- **Locale formatters are shared** (audit PRF-4). Number, date, plural and relative-time formatters are built once per locale and options, the time zone included, for the whole page, instead of once per call or per locale object: a number cell formats in about 0.6µs instead of 17µs, `formatPlural` in 1µs instead of 30µs, and a LedgerProvider created again reuses them along with its hour cycle and first weekday. The words are unchanged. See Components/Locale Formatters.
- **Toolbar measures only when its row changes** (audit PRF-6, TLB-8). One ResizeObserver lives as long as the toolbar and follows its groups as they mount and fold; the fold is measured before paint when the fold, More, or the row's words and elements change, and on the frame after a resize. The saved views' natural width is read again only when their content or the fonts change. A keystroke in the search of the app's My work register went from five layouts, a new observer, 16 rect reads and 8 computed-style reads to one layout and none of the rest. The fold, the focus hand-off and the DOM order are unchanged. See Patterns/Toolbar Live, Frame and Constrained.
- **A splitter drag keeps its width once** (audit PRF-8). While the pointer drags a Shell splitter, the width follows it on the Shell root's own style, and the shell's state, the `persist` storage and the document's stored-width variables are written once, when the pointer lets go (a cancelled gesture puts the width back first). A width set by a key, a default or a reset is kept at once, as before. Ten pointer moves on the app's side nav went from ten storage writes, ten document restyles and 1,500 component renders (170 of them SideNavItem) to none during the drag and one commit at the end. The panel's column no longer eases behind the pointer during a drag: the grid's open and close transition (240ms) is held off until the pointer lets go, so the panel's edge stays under the pointer, and opening or closing the panel still animates. See Layout/Shell Right To Left Splitters and Persisted Widths.
- **Shell.Aside starts its outline at 2** (audit G2-5, REC-16; D7). The aside is portalled beside Main, so it sets the heading level for what it holds: an Inspector group or a Section placed directly inside is an h2 under the page's h1 wherever the route renders it, and a Details rail no longer skips from h1 to h3. See Layout/Shell Record rail.
- **Sparklines draw in place** (audit PRF-11). A sparkline no longer animates its marks on mount or on a change of data: it sits in rows and tiles by the dozen, where the entrance cost a render per animation frame for each (the six in the In rows story went from 53 commits and 4,506 component renders to 5 and 438). A named sparkline's tooltip still follows the pointer. See Patterns/Chart/Sparkline In rows.
- **A fill register ignores other animations** (audit PRF-14). `useFillWindow` reads the block's top again after an `animationend` only when the animation ran on the block or a box around it (the page's entrance), not after every menu, tooltip or toast in the document. See Patterns/DataTable Fills the window.

- **FileTrigger** (audit GAP-7, STA-3, PA1-20; missing.md "FileTrigger and DropZone"). New component: a Button that opens the browser's file picker, with `accept` (a string or a list), `multiple`, `capture` and `onSelect(files)`. The native input is in the document, `hidden`: no space, no focus, no accessible role. The input is emptied after each choice, so the same file chosen again calls `onSelect` again. Inside a Field the button is the Field's control: the FieldLabel points at it (a click on the label opens the picker), it is named by the label then its own words ("Signed agreement Choose a file", with "(required)" for a required Field), the FieldDescription and FieldError describe it, and the Field's `invalid` and `disabled` reach it. Every Button prop applies (`isLoading`, `disabledReason`, `iconBefore`). The label defaults to the new `chooseFile` or `chooseFiles` message. In a ButtonGroup it is a segment like any Button: the hidden input carries no `data-slot`, so the group still rounds the trigger at its end. See Components/FileTrigger In a Field, Chosen files as Attachments, States and In a button group.
- **DropZone** (audit GAP-7, STA-3, STA-2; missing.md "FileTrigger and DropZone"). New component: a drop region with a FileTrigger inside for the keyboard, a screen reader and touch. Every drop and choice is checked against `accept`, `maxSize` (bytes per file) and `multiple`; accepted files go to `onSelect`, refusals to `onReject` with a `reason` (`type`, `size`, `count`) and a locale `message`, shown in the zone as the Field's FieldError (so the trigger is described by it and `aria-invalid`), and the result is announced politely ("Added plan.pdf. notes.docx is not an accepted file type."). Dragging files over it turns the border solid and the title to "Drop the file to add it", so the state is not colour alone; a drag of text does not light it; a disabled zone still takes the drag so the browser does not open the file. A click on the surface opens the picker for a pointer; the trigger stays the one tab stop. `title`, `triggerLabel`, `triggerRef` and `icon` adjust the content. A refusal marks the trigger `aria-invalid` even under a Field's explicit `invalid={false}`, and outside a Field it still describes the trigger. `accept="*/*"` takes any file. While dragging, the hint takes the subtle text colour, since the subtlest is under 4.5:1 on the selected surface in dark mode. New messages: `dropFileHere`, `dropFilesHere`, `dropFileRelease`, `dropFilesRelease`, `fileTypeRejected`, `fileTooLarge`, `fileCountRejected`, `fileAdded`, `filesAddedOne`, `filesAddedOther`. See Components/DropZone Validation, Drag and drop, Dragging, Without a Field, Disabled and Upload flow (Attachment rows uploading with Progress and Cancel, failing with Retry and Remove, done with Download; primary enabled, checked on submit).
- **Attachment.Link** (audit STA-12). A card-wide link: an anchor with the Trigger's overlay that keeps `href`, `download`, `target` and `rel`, forwards its ref and takes `render` for a router link, so no page copies the overlay classes onto an anchor. The Images and Group examples use it. See Components/Attachment Links and downloads.
- **An interactive Attachment answers hover and press** (audit STA-8). While its Trigger or Link is hovered (on a pointer that can hover) the card takes the raised hover surface, and the pressed surface while pressed; a disabled Trigger does neither, and hovering an action leaves the card at rest. See Components/Attachment Links and downloads.
- **Attachment.Title keeps the end of a long name** (audit STA-7). A plain-text name too long for the card is cut in the middle: the extension and the ten characters before it stay whole, so two scans that differ only in their date stay apart; assistive technology reads the name once, unbroken. `truncate="end"` restores the end cut, which a Title of elements always uses. Copying a selection inside the title gives the name once, as written, with no line break between the two pieces, and a name keeps its own direction on a right-to-left page. Attachment.mdx now says to put the full name in the Trigger's or Link's `title`, since the overlay covers the Title. See Components/Attachment Long names.
- **Attachment upload states cancel and announce** (audit STA-13). The uploading example has a Cancel named for the file that hands focus to its replacement, and every result goes through `announce`; the Accessibility section says so. See Components/Attachment Upload states.
- **Attachment parts by name.** `AttachmentMedia`, `AttachmentContent`, `AttachmentTitle`, `AttachmentDescription`, `AttachmentActions`, `AttachmentAction`, `AttachmentTrigger`, `AttachmentLink` and `AttachmentGroup` are exported beside the compound, so their props tables generate.
- **formatFileSize** (audit STA-9). `formatFileSize(bytes, { locale })`: "512 bytes", "840 kB", "2.4 MB", in decimal units with at most one decimal and the locale's numerals (en-US by default, never the host's). DropZone formats its size limit with it. See Components/Attachment File sizes.
- **downloadBlob and downloadText** (audit PRT-15; missing.md "File download helper"). Save a Blob or a string as a file through a hidden anchor, revoking the object URL after `DOWNLOAD_REVOKE_DELAY` (40 seconds), never in the same task as the click; `downloadText` takes a `type` and `bom: true` for a CSV a spreadsheet should read as UTF-8. Both return false and do nothing without a document. See Components/Attachment Links and downloads and Components/DropZone Upload flow.

- **Responsive audit, second pass** (`docs/guides/responsive-audit-2026-09-24.md`). Parts respond to the space they are given, fold instead of piling up on a phone, and work without hover.
  - **Stat.Grid** takes its columns from its own width (`stat-grid`, src/styles/stat.css). It shows `cols` while tiles keep at least 128px and folds in balanced steps otherwise: six and five to three, every count to two, one column below 257px. A 320px panel and a 320px phone show two tiles across. A short last row shares its width, and a parent sized to its content makes the grid as wide as its tiles. See Components/Stat, In A Narrow Panel and Sized To Its Content.
  - **Calendar** puts two months side by side only when they fit its own space. A range in a 320px panel stacks; a popover shows two months on a desktop and one column on a phone. See Components/Calendar, In A Narrow Panel, Range In A Popover and Range In A Popover On A Phone.
  - **Pagination** shows its Previous and Next words from 384px of its own width, and its list wraps. At the end of a row it takes the rest with `min-w-0 flex-1 justify-end`. See Components/Pagination, In A Narrow Panel and At The End Of A Row.
  - **WorkPane** puts its list beside the detail from 768px of pane. `sticky-rail` reads `--rail-top` and `--rail-max-height`, so a WorkPane inside a dialog or panel sticks to its own scroller. **Grid** keeps its viewport breakpoints; see Primitives/Grid, Intrinsic In A Panel.
  - **Headers.** Section.Header wraps on PageHeader's rule. The heading keeps a readable measure (12rem; 14rem for PageHeader), or its own width when shorter, and grows. Long titles move the actions to the next row, at the end; short titles keep them on the row. CardHeader with a CardAction keeps the action beside the title from about 18rem of card and gives it its own row below that. Inspector.Group and ActionBar follow suit. See Layout/Section Composed, Composed on a small phone and In a narrow panel; Layout/PageHeader Stacked; Components/Card On a small phone and In a rail.
  - **Toolbar** folds instead of stacking. When its container cannot hold the row, `filters` move into More first, then the display controls in `children` (Columns, Settings, an overflow of collection commands), which still open their own menus from there. The search, `views` and `actions` never fold, and the primary keeps its whole label. If the row still cannot fit, it wraps into two rows while More and the primary share a line; where they cannot (a long primary on a phone, a panel at its narrowest), the actions take a line of their own under More. More, its popover and the Display group take their names from the locale. No props changed. See Patterns/Toolbar Phone, Phone long primary, In a 320px frame, In a 240px frame, In a panel at its narrowest and Localized.
  - **Breadcrumb** keeps one line in any container. `BreadcrumbList` takes `overflow` (default `"collapse"`). When the trail does not fit, the middle levels fold behind the ellipsis, whose menu ("Show hidden levels") lists them as real links. Then the parent's and page's names share the rest, down to 80px each. The line keeps its height as levels fold and never starts or ends with a separator. `"wrap"` keeps each separator with the level it leads to. `BreadcrumbListProps` is exported. See Components/Breadcrumb Collapses To Fit, Collapses On Phone, Beside An Action, Composed Levels and Long Wrapping Trail.
  - **Tables work without hover.** Where nothing can hover, the DataTable row-actions kebab, the preview eye, and a header's column menu and grip are always shown. The value beside the eye ends in an ellipsis before it, and every table control under 24px takes a 24px hit area on a coarse pointer. DataTable pins give way while they would take more than 60% of a narrow frame and return as it widens; the reader's pins and exports do not change. A reorderable column's menu adds Move left and Move right: by keyboard, on a touch screen, or for anyone who cannot drag. The moves step among the columns a responsive row draws, and a polite status says where the column now stands. `Table.Header` and `Table.Cell` take `minWidth`. When `DataTable.Presets variant="menu"` runs out of room, its label ends in an ellipsis. See Patterns/Data table Pinned columns in a narrow frame, Row controls at 390px, Moving a column from its menu and Moving columns in a narrow frame, and Components/Table Register at 390px.
  - **InputGroup** never paints outside itself. A keyboard hint (an InputGroupText holding a Kbd) steps aside below 256px, and squeezed addons clip at the group's edge. See Components/InputGroup In a narrow frame. The Shell documents the top-nav search on a phone: a Search icon button below `md` opens the same search. See Layout/Shell Search at 320px.
  - **FilterChip** stays on one line; a long value truncates and keeps its full text as the title. **ToggleGroup** wraps a spaced group onto the next line when its row is too narrow. A joined group, a group in a table cell and a group in a horizontal Scroller keep one line. See Components/FilterChip In a 320px frame and Components/ToggleGroup Narrow row.
  - **Touch targets.** A control drawn under 24px takes an invisible hit area of at least 24px wherever any pointer is coarse. `touch-target` is a centred box; `touch-target-block` and `touch-target-block-after` are a band exactly the control's width, for links that wrap. They are applied to link buttons, dense IconButtons, badge links, Tree and Item chevrons, Related titles, table controls, breadcrumb links, TextLinks, Editable values and the Shell splitters. The splitters start a resize only on a sideways drag, and the panel's stays in place while its content scrolls. A KeyValue value truncates across only (`overflow-x: clip`), so a control inside keeps its focus ring and hit area. An IconButton's tooltip no longer reopens when focus returns from the menu or dialog it opened. See Button On touch, IconButton In a dense row and Opens A Menu, TextLink In prose, Editable In table and Rail, and KeyValue Control In A Value.
  - **Scroller** keeps the current item (a selected tab, the current step) in view when the strip first overflows or narrows, without moving focus. See Components/Scroller Keeps current in view.
  - **Charts.** Chart stories are fluid. `Chart.Donut` treats `size` as its largest diameter and scales down in a narrower container, and `Chart.Sparkline` treats `width` as its maximum. Reference and band labels above a plot move apart instead of overlapping, stay within the plot, and shorten only as far as the row needs. See Patterns/Chart/Donut Narrow, Sparkline Narrow and Line Narrow labels.
  - **Stories are checked narrow.** `npm run test:layout` renders every story at a 390px phone (storybook-narrow) and in a 320px container on the desktop canvas (storybook-contained). A story fails when the page scrolls sideways, a word breaks mid-word, or something paints past the container outside a scroller. The Storybook toolbar's Frame axis shows any story in a 320px frame. Deliberate overflows are listed in test/layout-allow.json. Fixture stories that overflowed because of a fixed width are fluid, the docs' Do/Don't pairs follow their own width, and wide docs tables scroll inside themselves. The package's unit tests cover how pins give way (`yieldPins`).

- Table cells given a `width` keep it: the value is also the cell's minimum, so a table wider than its frame no longer shrinks the checkbox, handle and detail columns below the 32px that DataTable adds to every pinned start offset, which left a slit beside a column pinned from its header menu and put its hairline past its edge. The row-actions column draws its hairline only while a column is scrolled under it, like the leading columns, instead of ruling off an empty gutter in a table that fits. Every toolbar in the Data table stories and docs wires search through the Toolbar's `search` and `onSearch`, so it comes first in the row; `DataTable.Search` as a Toolbar child sat among the end controls. See Patterns/Data table's Register (pin Finding, then scroll), Group by menu and Fill.

- Close the final interaction audit: tree tables have a maintained keyboard entry row; invalid or composing inline edits do not advance; filter choices follow updated rows and searches; pending record confirmation blocks dismissal and cannot close a later session. See Patterns/DataTable's Tree, Editing and Live Filters, and Patterns/RecordBrowser's Pending Confirmation.
- Preserve keyed child state when Inline inserts separators, and share hidden-series and table-view state across chart expansion. Treemap leaves expose named keyboard actions with Enter/Space and restore focus after details close. See Primitives/Inline's Separator And Spread, Patterns/Chart/Overview's Framed, and Patterns/Chart/Treemap's Drilldown and Details.
- Preserve control boundaries and selected states in forced colors for Switch, RadioGroup, Tabs and Progress; test those families in a dedicated browser project. Switch thumbs use the new `color.background.input.thumb` and `.checked` tokens without relying on optional base styles, and Banner action focus rings inherit the contrasting foreground. See Components/Switch's Switch Matrix, RadioGroup's Orientation, Tabs' Variants, Progress' Progress Matrix and Banner's Banners. Correct the documented input-border contrast policy and five stale family links.
- Fix Calendar's around navigation and RTL chevrons, keep the command palette footer within short viewports, make both shell splitters follow physical pointer and arrow movement in RTL, and preserve saved desktop widths across narrow-screen reloads. See Components/Calendar's Navigation Layouts, Components/Command's Short Viewport, and Layout/Shell's Persisted Widths and Right To Left Splitters.
- Keep a focused item visible within Scroller's own axis, including RTL strips, and compose scrolling navigation in the compact queue example. Contain the motion-token demonstration's intentional offscreen animation. See Components/Scroller's Horizontal, Layout/Pages' Compact Queue and Tokens/Motion's Preference.

- **Responsive audit fixes** (`docs/guides/responsive-audit-2026-09-18.md`). PageHeader is a wrapping row: the heading keeps a 14rem measure and grows, and when a row cannot hold that beside the actions they take the next row, right-aligned, on a page, in a panel and in a sheet alike; the panel-only stacking rule is gone (Layout/PageHeader Stacked). Below the aside breakpoint, and beside an open panel below the wide one, the shell's Main row is `auto` and the aside's row takes the rest of the window, so a short record's rail follows its content instead of starting a screen down (Layout/Shell Record rail at 390px). `Shell.TopNav.End` takes `overflow`, one menu that stands in for its children below `md` (the same story). Horizontal Stepper and Timeline keep their 420px and scroll inside a Scroller below it (Components/Stepper Narrow, Components/Timeline Narrow). PageSkeleton's head lines follow the page's width (Layout/PageSkeleton Narrow). The Toolbar's `views` row and the DataTable.Presets strip scroll inside their row when it cannot hold them; the Toolbar measures a strip by what it holds (Patterns/Data table Register at 340px). KeyValue `wrap` breaks an unbroken identifier or timestamp (Components/KeyValue Unbroken). `dimension.breakpoint.sm` and `.xl` join the tokens so every `sm:`/`xl:` class and the toast's query read one source. The Storybook has Phone (390) and Small phone (340) viewports; the narrow stories carry them and every story now fails if it scrolls the page sideways at the width it renders at (Patterns/Chart/Overview Small Phone).

- Add `Scroller`, `ScrollerViewport` and `ScrollerArrow`: one bounded scrolling region with an arrow at each edge that can still scroll, vertical or horizontal, hover-scrolling (a quiet zone) or click-stepping (a button with a pointer cursor, hover tint and inner hairline), on a named surface, shown only where the pointer can hover, replacing the native scrollbar while they show, with scroll padding that keeps a focused item clear of the arrows. Select's arrows wear its look and now span the popup's inner box instead of overhanging it; DropdownMenu content and Combobox's List compose it; horizontal line Tabs get click arrows over their ScrollArea that carry the strip's hairline and replace its scrollbar and gutter while they show. Four locale messages name the click arrows. See Components/Scroller's Vertical and Horizontal, Components/Select's Scrolling (now on a left-to-right canvas with one RTL specimen), Components/DropdownMenu's Scrolling and Components/Tabs' Responsive widths.

- Give fields one continuous 2px focus edge in place of a border plus an offset ring. Input, Textarea, Select, DatePicker, InputGroup, Combobox and Calendar dropdowns retain their dimensions; invalid fields keep an error-colored edge. See Components/Input's Input Matrix, Components/Combobox's Multiple and Components/Calendar's Dropdowns And Week Numbers.

- Add five Guidance pages that answer by need rather than by family: Choosing a part, Coming from shadcn (`render` not `asChild`, tokens not the shadcn theme, a name-by-name map), Recipes (register, record, preview, form, states), Which token (the short token answer by job, with values) and Writing stories (this package's conventions beside the generic Storybook guidance). All flow into `llms.txt`.
- Add `AGENTS.md` (with `CLAUDE.md` importing it) as the package's entry point for coding agents, the Guidance/Agents Storybook page describing the MCP workflow, and `npm run build:llms`, which generates `llms.txt` from every Storybook page, the token sheet and the public export inventory. The package build refreshes it and `test/llms.test.mjs` fails on drift. `llms.txt` and `AGENTS.md` ship in the tarball.

- Let Shell panel header slots respond to panel width: narrow titles receive a full row, with controls kept together, and nested PageHeader actions reflow at the smallest width. Title-only headers retain their compact Close placement. See Layout/Shell Panel header and Panel record header examples.

- Add `DataTable responsive` and column `priority`: container-based fitting with labelled row disclosures preserves column preferences and exports. Honour explicit id-column minimum widths. See Patterns/DataTable Responsive containers. App-only lint rules `product-responsive-table` and `product-line-tabs` guard the composition without restricting generic kit variants.

- Make horizontal line tabs fill their container with one continuous border and one row of tabs. `TabsList` composes the existing ScrollArea and horizontal ScrollBar; remove caller width, overflow and wrapping overrides. Filled and vertical variants retain their layout, and native list props/refs stay on the tablist. See Components/Tabs' Responsive widths, Variants and Orientation stories for page, phone, preview and keyboard scrolling checks.

- Add `PreviewNavigation` with localized previous/next, position and a caller-owned real full-record link. See Patterns/PreviewNavigation's Collection, Outside results and Localized stories; Layout/Pages' Queue with panel uses the same control.
- Add `Toolbar.views` for permanent saved-view controls after Search and before overflowable filters; Columns, Settings and actions remain visible at narrow widths. See Patterns/Toolbar's Constrained and Patterns/DataTable's register examples.
- Separate `PreviewSheet`'s outer global navigation from its inner record header. Back, `navigation` and Close stay in the outer header; `actions` now renders in the inner `PageHeader.Actions` beside the accessible `SheetTitle`, with metadata below. `openTo` is the outer fallback only when navigation is absent; related links alone may use the footer. Existing props and focus overrides remain supported. See Patterns/PreviewSheet's Collection task, Preview sheet and Complete review slot, modal-focus and frame-navigation checks.
- Add recommended ESLint rules `no-native-confirm`, `text-link-navigation` and `dialog-footer-order`, with focused valid/invalid composition fixtures. Reconcile Forms, Overlays, Toolbar and PreviewSheet guidance with the application's documented workflow exceptions.

- Fix `no-kit-shadow` to use a generated inventory of actual public value exports, including layout and named exports such as PageHeader and Badge. Private implementation names and type-only exports are excluded. The package build updates the inventory, regression tests reject drift, and installed ESLint consumers need neither TypeScript nor component source.

- **Layout follow-up** (`docs/guides/layout-followup-plan.md`). `tokenValue` returns the token's light value (`tokenLiterals`) without a document or while the stylesheet is not loaded, so the shell's media queries are never empty. What `persist` restores is validated (`parseStoredShell`) and clamped to the screen it is restored on. The flyout's close timer is cleared on unmount. The splitters take Home (the minimum) and End (the maximum) beside the arrows, and their value text is a locale message. Every default name in the shell (Banner, Top navigation, Side navigation, Main content, Page context, Details, Actions, Switch product, the toggle's labels, the skip links' heading) is a locale message and no longer lowercased in a skip link. Every optional prop in the layer is spelled `?: T | undefined`, so `label={maybe}` typechecks under `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`. One prop order in every part: defaults, then the consumer's props, then the part's identity (`data-slot`, landmark role, `id`, `tabIndex`, the accessible name it computes), so a stray prop cannot un-landmark an area; the `render` parts merge the same way. `SideNavTrigger` is exported. The panel header uses the generated `min-h-layout-topnav` (`min-h-layout-*` exists for every layout height). Internally `shell.tsx` is a folder (`context`, `root`, `top-nav`, `side-nav`, `splitter`, `panel`) and the navigation system's CSS is `shell.css` beside `layout.css`; exports are unchanged. The compact-mode rule hides the panel's splitter by its `data-slot`, not by `role`, so a Divider placed directly in a composed panel stays. New stories: Layout/Shell's Side nav shortcut, Skip links, Banner toggle and Labelled panel, Layout/Section's Untitled; Frame covers Home and End. The Shell, PageHeader and Section pages carry props tables for their parts. The app's nine hand-written lead lines are `PageHeader.Lead`.

- The shell's panel is the full height of the window. From 1280px it takes the last grid column across every row, so the banner and the top nav stop at its edge and opening it narrows the whole page instead of the page body alone; below 1280px it still replaces Main under the top nav. Its header is the top nav's height, so beside it the two hairlines are one line, and a title that wraps still grows it. The Shell stories move under Layout. See Layout/Shell's With banner and panel and Layout/Pages' Queue with panel.

- **The layout layer on parts, every part forwarding.** Every Shell part, PageHeader, Section and PageSkeleton render one element and forward the native props and the ref to it, stamped `data-slot`; `Profile` is a menu's trigger through `render` (its job-title prop is `description`; `role`, which collides with the ARIA attribute, stays as a deprecated alias), `AppSwitcher` and `SideNav.ToggleButton` forward IconButton's props. `Shell.Panel` gains `Panel.Header`, `Panel.Title` (h2, or `render` for another level; it names the landmark), `Panel.Actions`, `Panel.Close`, `Panel.Body` and `Panel.Splitter`; `title` and `actions` still render the built-in header. `PageHeader.Lead` (a breadcrumb or category line across both columns) and `PageHeader.Heading` (the first column) replace hand-written `col-span-full` and `min-w-0` wrappers. `Section.Header`, `Section.Heading`, `Section.Title` (`render` for the level), `Section.Description` and `Section.Actions` compose a section by hand; `title` is optional and still renders the built-in header. The `SideNav.ToggleButton` is a child of `TopNav.Start` and places itself; `toggle` stays as sugar. `TopNav.End` no longer folds its children into a More popover (they were rendered twice), so `moreLabel` is gone and a product renders its own narrow-screen menu; the app does. Side nav icons accept an element as well as a constructor (`SideNavIcon`, `IconComponent`). Splitter labels are optional and, with the skip links, the scrim and the panel's close, come from the locale's messages. `SideNavSlotProps` is deprecated for `SideNavHeaderProps`, `SideNavBodyProps` and `SideNavFooterProps`. The stacking order is three variables on `.shell-root`. The breakpoints are tokens, `dimension.breakpoint.{md,lg,aside,panel,wide}`: the build writes them as literal `--breakpoint-*` theme keys, so `layout.css` reads `theme(--breakpoint-panel)`, the shell reads `tokenValue("dimension.breakpoint.panel")`, and `aside:`, `panel:` and `wide:` variants exist beside `md:` and `lg:`; Metrics lists them. See Layout/Shell's Composed panel and Forwarding, Layout/PageHeader's Record and Layout/Section's Composed.

- Shell fixes from the layout composability audit (`docs/guides/layout-composability-audit.md`). The Ctrl+[ guard, the flyout's hold and its click-outside test looked for Radix attributes the kit no longer emits; they read Base UI's `data-open`, `data-popup-open` and the kit's `data-slot="…-portal"`. A side nav the browser remembered collapsed could not fly out, because the before-paint attribute was put back after every change and its unlayered `display: none` beat the flyout; React now removes it once it has restored the state. `TopNav.End` renders its children as given instead of wrapping whatever `Children.toArray` found in list items, so a component that returns several buttons is several buttons. `defaultWidth` on SideNav and Panel applies only while no width is set, before paint, so a dragged or remembered width survives a panel re-mount instead of being reset to the default. See Layout/Shell's Remembered.

- Add `fill` to Table and DataTable, the `fill-window` layout utility and `useFillWindow`: a register that is the page's one block takes the rest of the window, its header sticks to its frame, its rows scroll inside and its pagination sits at the bottom of Main's inset (`--shell-inset-bottom`). `fill` wins over `maxHeight`. See Data table's Fills the window, Table's Fill frame and Pages' Register.

- Add Rows per page to the DataTable's Pagination: `useDataTable` takes `pageSizes` (`10, 20, 50, 100` unsaid, `pageSize` always among them), the choice persists with `view` and Reset view returns it. The renderer reads the page size from the table's state. See Data table's Fills the window.

- Table.Header draws its hairline on the cell (`hairline`, off for a placeholder), so it stays under the heading while the frame scrolls; a collapsed border stayed with the rows. See Table's Fill frame.

- Refine field and surface states: invalid labels match error messages, choice cards gain enabled hover and quiet disabled surfaces, and labeled dividers work on any background. InputGroup follows its control's state, so a disabled addon no longer dims an editable input; focused invalid controls retain their error border. Card uses shared vertical spacing with optional `border-b`/`border-t` rules on header/footer. Edge-to-edge table examples opt into their divider and flush spacing. Native props and compact control sizes are unchanged.

- Align app and Storybook font loading to Geist and Geist Mono. Refine button icon insets and expanded-popup states. Select, Combobox and menu rows share compact minimum sizing and grow for rich content; committed choices remain distinct from hover/keyboard highlight. Editable.Select preserves custom rendering in searchable options while keeping string-based search and accessible names. See Button, Editable/Roster and Layout/Pages/Record.

- Add [RecordBrowser](src/stories/patterns/RecordBrowser.mdx): a 90% viewport relationship chooser with a searchable, filterable table, independent record preview, persistent multi-selection and explicit confirmation. Preview navigation and Escape restore focus within the dialog; failed linking preserves the selection. `Shell.Panel.actions` accepts route-owned previous/next and full-record navigation beside Close.

- Toolbar collapses only its explicit `filters` slot; view controls and actions stay visible. Header actions remain beside wrapping titles at all widths. Editable.Text supports multiline cell editing with newline, commit and cancel keyboard behavior.

- Keep Toolbar on one row by moving filters and actions into a Base UI popover when its container narrows; preserve search, filter state and keyboard focus across resizing. PageHeader examples keep fields in the work area and use a single Actions menu. Shell.Panel contains horizontal overflow while nested scroll areas remain usable. See Toolbar’s Constrained story and the layout examples.

- Separate layout from reusable interaction. Mount the application shell once at the router root; routes contribute context and selected work through stable `Shell.Aside` / `Shell.Panel` slots.
